    "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/cryptography/ECDSA.sol": {
      "content": "// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\n// OpenZeppelin Contracts (last updated v4.9.0) (utils/cryptography/ECDSA.sol)\n\npragma solidity ^0.8.0;\n\nimport \"../Strings.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @dev Elliptic Curve Digital Signature Algorithm (ECDSA) operations.\n *\n * These functions can be used to verify that a message was signed by the holder\n * of the private keys of a given address.\n */\nlibrary ECDSA {\n    enum RecoverError {\n        NoError,\n        InvalidSignature,\n        InvalidSignatureLength,\n        InvalidSignatureS,\n        InvalidSignatureV // Deprecated in v4.8\n    }\n\n    function _throwError(RecoverError error) private pure {\n        if (error == RecoverError.NoError) {\n            return; // no error: do nothing\n        } else if (error == RecoverError.InvalidSignature) {\n            revert(\"ECDSA: invalid signature\");\n        } else if (error == RecoverError.InvalidSignatureLength) {\n            revert(\"ECDSA: invalid signature length\");\n        } else if (error == RecoverError.InvalidSignatureS) {\n            revert(\"ECDSA: invalid signature 's' value\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Returns the address that signed a hashed message (`hash`) with\n     * `signature` or error string. This address can then be used for verification purposes.\n     *\n     * The `ecrecover` EVM opcode allows for malleable (non-unique) signatures:\n     * this function rejects them by requiring the `s` value to be in the lower\n     * half order, and the `v` value to be either 27 or 28.\n     *\n     * IMPORTANT: `hash` _must_ be the result of a hash operation for the\n     * verification to be secure: it is possible to craft signatures that\n     * recover to arbitrary addresses for non-hashed data. A safe way to ensure\n     * this is by receiving a hash of the original message (which may otherwise\n     * be too long), and then calling {toEthSignedMessageHash} on it.\n     *\n     * Documentation for signature generation:\n     * - with https://web3js.readthedocs.io/en/v1.3.4/web3-eth-accounts.html#sign[Web3.js]\n     * - with https://docs.ethers.io/v5/api/signer/#Signer-signMessage[ethers]\n     *\n     * _Available since v4.3._\n     */\n    function tryRecover(bytes32 hash, bytes memory signature) internal pure returns (address, RecoverError) {\n        if (signature.length == 65) {\n            bytes32 r;\n            bytes32 s;\n            uint8 v;\n            // ecrecover takes the signature parameters, and the only way to get them\n            // currently is to use assembly.\n            /// @solidity memory-safe-assembly\n            assembly {\n                r := mload(add(signature, 0x20))\n                s := mload(add(signature, 0x40))\n                v := byte(0, mload(add(signature, 0x60)))\n            }\n            return tryRecover(hash, v, r, s);\n        } else {\n            return (address(0), RecoverError.InvalidSignatureLength);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Returns the address that signed a hashed message (`hash`) with\n     * `signature`. This address can then be used for verification purposes.\n     *\n     * The `ecrecover` EVM opcode allows for malleable (non-unique) signatures:\n     * this function rejects them by requiring the `s` value to be in the lower\n     * half order, and the `v` value to be either 27 or 28.\n     *\n     * IMPORTANT: `hash` _must_ be the result of a hash operation for the\n     * verification to be secure: it is possible to craft signatures that\n     * recover to arbitrary addresses for non-hashed data. A safe way to ensure\n     * this is by receiving a hash of the original message (which may otherwise\n     * be too long), and then calling {toEthSignedMessageHash} on it.\n     */\n    function recover(bytes32 hash, bytes memory signature) internal pure returns (address) {\n        (address recovered, RecoverError error) = tryRecover(hash, signature);\n        _throwError(error);\n        return recovered;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Overload of {ECDSA-tryRecover} that receives the `r` and `vs` short-signature fields separately.\n     *\n     * See https://eips.ethereum.org/EIPS/eip-2098[EIP-2098 short signatures]\n     *\n     * _Available since v4.3._\n     */\n    function tryRecover(bytes32 hash, bytes32 r, bytes32 vs) internal pure returns (address, RecoverError) {\n        bytes32 s = vs & bytes32(0x7fffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffff);\n        uint8 v = uint8((uint256(vs) >> 255) + 27);\n        return tryRecover(hash, v, r, s);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Overload of {ECDSA-recover} that receives the `r and `vs` short-signature fields separately.\n     *\n     * _Available since v4.2._\n     */\n    function recover(bytes32 hash, bytes32 r, bytes32 vs) internal pure returns (address) {\n        (address recovered, RecoverError error) = tryRecover(hash, r, vs);\n        _throwError(error);\n        return recovered;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Overload of {ECDSA-tryRecover} that receives the `v`,\n     * `r` and `s` signature fields separately.\n     *\n     * _Available since v4.3._\n     */\n    function tryRecover(bytes32 hash, uint8 v, bytes32 r, bytes32 s) internal pure returns (address, RecoverError) {\n        // EIP-2 still allows signature malleability for ecrecover(). Remove this possibility and make the signature\n        // unique. Appendix F in the Ethereum Yellow paper (https://ethereum.github.io/yellowpaper/paper.pdf), defines\n        // the valid range for s in (301): 0 < s < secp256k1n ÷ 2 + 1, and for v in (302): v ∈ {27, 28}. Most\n        // signatures from current libraries generate a unique signature with an s-value in the lower half order.\n        //\n        // If your library generates malleable signatures, such as s-values in the upper range, calculate a new s-value\n        // with 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFEBAAEDCE6AF48A03BBFD25E8CD0364141 - s1 and flip v from 27 to 28 or\n        // vice versa. If your library also generates signatures with 0/1 for v instead 27/28, add 27 to v to accept\n        // these malleable signatures as well.\n        if (uint256(s) > 0x7FFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFF5D576E7357A4501DDFE92F46681B20A0) {\n            return (address(0), RecoverError.InvalidSignatureS);\n        }\n\n        // If the signature is valid (and not malleable), return the signer address\n        address signer = ecrecover(hash, v, r, s);\n        if (signer == address(0)) {\n            return (address(0), RecoverError.InvalidSignature);\n        }\n\n        return (signer, RecoverError.NoError);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Overload of {ECDSA-recover} that receives the `v`,\n     * `r` and `s` signature fields separately.\n     */\n    function recover(bytes32 hash, uint8 v, bytes32 r, bytes32 s) internal pure returns (address) {\n        (address recovered, RecoverError error) = tryRecover(hash, v, r, s);\n        _throwError(error);\n        return recovered;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Returns an Ethereum Signed Message, created from a `hash`. This\n     * produces hash corresponding to the one signed with the\n     * https://eth.wiki/json-rpc/API#eth_sign[`eth_sign`]\n     * JSON-RPC method as part of EIP-191.\n     *\n     * See {recover}.\n     */\n    function toEthSignedMessageHash(bytes32 hash) internal pure returns (bytes32 message) {\n        // 32 is the length in bytes of hash,\n        // enforced by the type signature above\n        /// @solidity memory-safe-assembly\n        assembly {\n            mstore(0x00, \"\\x19Ethereum Signed Message:\\n32\")\n            mstore(0x1c, hash)\n            message := keccak256(0x00, 0x3c)\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Returns an Ethereum Signed Message, created from `s`. This\n     * produces hash corresponding to the one signed with the\n     * https://eth.wiki/json-rpc/API#eth_sign[`eth_sign`]\n     * JSON-RPC method as part of EIP-191.\n     *\n     * See {recover}.\n     */\n    function toEthSignedMessageHash(bytes memory s) internal pure returns (bytes32) {\n        return keccak256(abi.encodePacked(\"\\x19Ethereum Signed Message:\\n\", Strings.toString(s.length), s));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Returns an Ethereum Signed Typed Data, created from a\n     * `domainSeparator` and a `structHash`. This produces hash corresponding\n     * to the one signed with the\n     * https://eips.ethereum.org/EIPS/eip-712[`eth_signTypedData`]\n     * JSON-RPC method as part of EIP-712.\n     *\n     * See {recover}.\n     */\n    function toTypedDataHash(bytes32 domainSeparator, bytes32 structHash) internal pure returns (bytes32 data) {\n        /// @solidity memory-safe-assembly\n        assembly {\n            let ptr := mload(0x40)\n            mstore(ptr, \"\\x19\\x01\")\n            mstore(add(ptr, 0x02), domainSeparator)\n            mstore(add(ptr, 0x22), structHash)\n            data := keccak256(ptr, 0x42)\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Returns an Ethereum Signed Data with intended validator, created from a\n     * `validator` and `data` according to the version 0 of EIP-191.\n     *\n     * See {recover}.\n     */\n    function toDataWithIntendedValidatorHash(address validator, bytes memory data) internal pure returns (bytes32) {\n        return keccak256(abi.encodePacked(\"\\x19\\x00\", validator, data));\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol": {
      "content": "// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\n// OpenZeppelin Contracts (last updated v4.9.0) (utils/math/Math.sol)\n\npragma solidity ^0.8.0;\n\n/**\n * @dev Standard math utilities missing in the Solidity language.\n */\nlibrary Math {\n    enum Rounding {\n        Down, // Toward negative infinity\n        Up, // Toward infinity\n        Zero // Toward zero\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Returns the largest of two numbers.\n     */\n    function max(uint256 a, uint256 b) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        return a > b ? a : b;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Returns the smallest of two numbers.\n     */\n    function min(uint256 a, uint256 b) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        return a < b ? a : b;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Returns the average of two numbers. The result is rounded towards\n     * zero.\n     */\n    function average(uint256 a, uint256 b) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        // (a + b) / 2 can overflow.\n        return (a & b) + (a ^ b) / 2;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Returns the ceiling of the division of two numbers.\n     *\n     * This differs from standard division with `/` in that it rounds up instead\n     * of rounding down.\n     */\n    function ceilDiv(uint256 a, uint256 b) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        // (a + b - 1) / b can overflow on addition, so we distribute.\n        return a == 0 ? 0 : (a - 1) / b + 1;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculates floor(x * y / denominator) with full precision. Throws if result overflows a uint256 or denominator == 0\n     * @dev Original credit to Remco Bloemen under MIT license (https://xn--2-umb.com/21/muldiv)\n     * with further edits by Uniswap Labs also under MIT license.\n     */\n    function mulDiv(uint256 x, uint256 y, uint256 denominator) internal pure returns (uint256 result) {\n        unchecked {\n            // 512-bit multiply [prod1 prod0] = x * y. Compute the product mod 2^256 and mod 2^256 - 1, then use\n            // use the Chinese Remainder Theorem to reconstruct the 512 bit result. The result is stored in two 256\n            // variables such that product = prod1 * 2^256 + prod0.\n            uint256 prod0; // Least significant 256 bits of the product\n            uint256 prod1; // Most significant 256 bits of the product\n            assembly {\n                let mm := mulmod(x, y, not(0))\n                prod0 := mul(x, y)\n                prod1 := sub(sub(mm, prod0), lt(mm, prod0))\n            }\n\n            // Handle non-overflow cases, 256 by 256 division.\n            if (prod1 == 0) {\n                // Solidity will revert if denominator == 0, unlike the div opcode on its own.\n                // The surrounding unchecked block does not change this fact.\n                // See https://docs.soliditylang.org/en/latest/control-structures.html#checked-or-unchecked-arithmetic.\n                return prod0 / denominator;\n            }\n\n            // Make sure the result is less than 2^256. Also prevents denominator == 0.\n            require(denominator > prod1, \"Math: mulDiv overflow\");\n\n            ///////////////////////////////////////////////\n            // 512 by 256 division.\n            ///////////////////////////////////////////////\n\n            // Make division exact by subtracting the remainder from [prod1 prod0].\n            uint256 remainder;\n            assembly {\n                // Compute remainder using mulmod.\n                remainder := mulmod(x, y, denominator)\n\n                // Subtract 256 bit number from 512 bit number.\n                prod1 := sub(prod1, gt(remainder, prod0))\n                prod0 := sub(prod0, remainder)\n            }\n\n            // Factor powers of two out of denominator and compute largest power of two divisor of denominator. Always >= 1.\n            // See https://cs.stackexchange.com/q/138556/92363.\n\n            // Does not overflow because the denominator cannot be zero at this stage in the function.\n            uint256 twos = denominator & (~denominator + 1);\n            assembly {\n                // Divide denominator by twos.\n                denominator := div(denominator, twos)\n\n                // Divide [prod1 prod0] by twos.\n                prod0 := div(prod0, twos)\n\n                // Flip twos such that it is 2^256 / twos. If twos is zero, then it becomes one.\n                twos := add(div(sub(0, twos), twos), 1)\n            }\n\n            // Shift in bits from prod1 into prod0.\n            prod0 |= prod1 * twos;\n\n            // Invert denominator mod 2^256. Now that denominator is an odd number, it has an inverse modulo 2^256 such\n            // that denominator * inv = 1 mod 2^256. Compute the inverse by starting with a seed that is correct for\n            // four bits. That is, denominator * inv = 1 mod 2^4.\n            uint256 inverse = (3 * denominator) ^ 2;\n\n            // Use the Newton-Raphson iteration to improve the precision. Thanks to Hensel's lifting lemma, this also works\n            // in modular arithmetic, doubling the correct bits in each step.\n            inverse *= 2 - denominator * inverse; // inverse mod 2^8\n            inverse *= 2 - denominator * inverse; // inverse mod 2^16\n            inverse *= 2 - denominator * inverse; // inverse mod 2^32\n            inverse *= 2 - denominator * inverse; // inverse mod 2^64\n            inverse *= 2 - denominator * inverse; // inverse mod 2^128\n            inverse *= 2 - denominator * inverse; // inverse mod 2^256\n\n            // Because the division is now exact we can divide by multiplying with the modular inverse of denominator.\n            // This will give us the correct result modulo 2^256. Since the preconditions guarantee that the outcome is\n            // less than 2^256, this is the final result. We don't need to compute the high bits of the result and prod1\n            // is no longer required.\n            result = prod0 * inverse;\n            return result;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculates x * y / denominator with full precision, following the selected rounding direction.\n     */\n    function mulDiv(uint256 x, uint256 y, uint256 denominator, Rounding rounding) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 result = mulDiv(x, y, denominator);\n        if (rounding == Rounding.Up && mulmod(x, y, denominator) > 0) {\n            result += 1;\n        }\n        return result;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Returns the square root of a number. If the number is not a perfect square, the value is rounded down.\n     *\n     * Inspired by Henry S. Warren, Jr.'s \"Hacker's Delight\" (Chapter 11).\n     */\n    function sqrt(uint256 a) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        if (a == 0) {\n            return 0;\n        }\n\n        // For our first guess, we get the biggest power of 2 which is smaller than the square root of the target.\n        //\n        // We know that the \"msb\" (most significant bit) of our target number `a` is a power of 2 such that we have\n        // `msb(a) <= a < 2*msb(a)`. This value can be written `msb(a)=2**k` with `k=log2(a)`.\n        //\n        // This can be rewritten `2**log2(a) <= a < 2**(log2(a) + 1)`\n        // → `sqrt(2**k) <= sqrt(a) < sqrt(2**(k+1))`\n        // → `2**(k/2) <= sqrt(a) < 2**((k+1)/2) <= 2**(k/2 + 1)`\n        //\n        // Consequently, `2**(log2(a) / 2)` is a good first approximation of `sqrt(a)` with at least 1 correct bit.\n        uint256 result = 1 << (log2(a) >> 1);\n\n        // At this point `result` is an estimation with one bit of precision. We know the true value is a uint128,\n        // since it is the square root of a uint256. Newton's method converges quadratically (precision doubles at\n        // every iteration). We thus need at most 7 iteration to turn our partial result with one bit of precision\n        // into the expected uint128 result.\n        unchecked {\n            result = (result + a / result) >> 1;\n            result = (result + a / result) >> 1;\n            result = (result + a / result) >> 1;\n            result = (result + a / result) >> 1;\n            result = (result + a / result) >> 1;\n            result = (result + a / result) >> 1;\n            result = (result + a / result) >> 1;\n            return min(result, a / result);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculates sqrt(a), following the selected rounding direction.\n     */\n    function sqrt(uint256 a, Rounding rounding) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        unchecked {\n            uint256 result = sqrt(a);\n            return result + (rounding == Rounding.Up && result * result < a ? 1 : 0);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Return the log in base 2, rounded down, of a positive value.\n     * Returns 0 if given 0.\n     */\n    function log2(uint256 value) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 result = 0;\n        unchecked {\n            if (value >> 128 > 0) {\n                value >>= 128;\n                result += 128;\n            }\n            if (value >> 64 > 0) {\n                value >>= 64;\n                result += 64;\n            }\n            if (value >> 32 > 0) {\n                value >>= 32;\n                result += 32;\n            }\n            if (value >> 16 > 0) {\n                value >>= 16;\n                result += 16;\n            }\n            if (value >> 8 > 0) {\n                value >>= 8;\n                result += 8;\n            }\n            if (value >> 4 > 0) {\n                value >>= 4;\n                result += 4;\n            }\n            if (value >> 2 > 0) {\n                value >>= 2;\n                result += 2;\n            }\n            if (value >> 1 > 0) {\n                result += 1;\n            }\n        }\n        return result;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Return the log in base 2, following the selected rounding direction, of a positive value.\n     * Returns 0 if given 0.\n     */\n    function log2(uint256 value, Rounding rounding) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        unchecked {\n            uint256 result = log2(value);\n            return result + (rounding == Rounding.Up && 1 << result < value ? 1 : 0);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Return the log in base 10, rounded down, of a positive value.\n     * Returns 0 if given 0.\n     */\n    function log10(uint256 value) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 result = 0;\n        unchecked {\n            if (value >= 10 ** 64) {\n                value /= 10 ** 64;\n                result += 64;\n            }\n            if (value >= 10 ** 32) {\n                value /= 10 ** 32;\n                result += 32;\n            }\n            if (value >= 10 ** 16) {\n                value /= 10 ** 16;\n                result += 16;\n            }\n            if (value >= 10 ** 8) {\n                value /= 10 ** 8;\n                result += 8;\n            }\n            if (value >= 10 ** 4) {\n                value /= 10 ** 4;\n                result += 4;\n            }\n            if (value >= 10 ** 2) {\n                value /= 10 ** 2;\n                result += 2;\n            }\n            if (value >= 10 ** 1) {\n                result += 1;\n            }\n        }\n        return result;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Return the log in base 10, following the selected rounding direction, of a positive value.\n     * Returns 0 if given 0.\n     */\n    function log10(uint256 value, Rounding rounding) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        unchecked {\n            uint256 result = log10(value);\n            return result + (rounding == Rounding.Up && 10 ** result < value ? 1 : 0);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Return the log in base 256, rounded down, of a positive value.\n     * Returns 0 if given 0.\n     *\n     * Adding one to the result gives the number of pairs of hex symbols needed to represent `value` as a hex string.\n     */\n    function log256(uint256 value) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 result = 0;\n        unchecked {\n            if (value >> 128 > 0) {\n                value >>= 128;\n                result += 16;\n            }\n            if (value >> 64 > 0) {\n                value >>= 64;\n                result += 8;\n            }\n            if (value >> 32 > 0) {\n                value >>= 32;\n                result += 4;\n            }\n            if (value >> 16 > 0) {\n                value >>= 16;\n                result += 2;\n            }\n            if (value >> 8 > 0) {\n                result += 1;\n            }\n        }\n        return result;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Return the log in base 256, following the selected rounding direction, of a positive value.\n     * Returns 0 if given 0.\n     */\n    function log256(uint256 value, Rounding rounding) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        unchecked {\n            uint256 result = log256(value);\n            return result + (rounding == Rounding.Up && 1 << (result << 3) < value ? 1 : 0);\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/SignedMath.sol": {
      "content": "// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\n// OpenZeppelin Contracts (last updated v4.8.0) (utils/math/SignedMath.sol)\n\npragma solidity ^0.8.0;\n\n/**\n * @dev Standard signed math utilities missing in the Solidity language.\n */\nlibrary SignedMath {\n    /**\n     * @dev Returns the largest of two signed numbers.\n     */\n    function max(int256 a, int256 b) internal pure returns (int256) {\n        return a > b ? a : b;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Returns the smallest of two signed numbers.\n     */\n    function min(int256 a, int256 b) internal pure returns (int256) {\n        return a < b ? a : b;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Returns the average of two signed numbers without overflow.\n     * The result is rounded towards zero.\n     */\n    function average(int256 a, int256 b) internal pure returns (int256) {\n        // Formula from the book \"Hacker's Delight\"\n        int256 x = (a & b) + ((a ^ b) >> 1);\n        return x + (int256(uint256(x) >> 255) & (a ^ b));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Returns the absolute unsigned value of a signed value.\n     */\n    function abs(int256 n) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        unchecked {\n            // must be unchecked in order to support `n = type(int256).min`\n            return uint256(n >= 0 ? n : -n);\n        }\n    }\n}\n"

      "content": "// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\n// OpenZeppelin Contracts (last updated v4.9.0) (utils/Strings.sol)\n\npragma solidity ^0.8.0;\n\nimport \"./math/Math.sol\";\nimport \"./math/SignedMath.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @dev String operations.\n */\nlibrary Strings {\n    bytes16 private constant _SYMBOLS = \"0123456789abcdef\";\n    uint8 private constant _ADDRESS_LENGTH = 20;\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Converts a `uint256` to its ASCII `string` decimal representation.\n     */\n    function toString(uint256 value) internal pure returns (string memory) {\n        unchecked {\n            uint256 length = Math.log10(value) + 1;\n            string memory buffer = new string(length);\n            uint256 ptr;\n            /// @solidity memory-safe-assembly\n            assembly {\n                ptr := add(buffer, add(32, length))\n            }\n            while (true) {\n                ptr--;\n                /// @solidity memory-safe-assembly\n                assembly {\n                    mstore8(ptr, byte(mod(value, 10), _SYMBOLS))\n                }\n                value /= 10;\n                if (value == 0) break;\n            }\n            return buffer;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Converts a `int256` to its ASCII `string` decimal representation.\n     */\n    function toString(int256 value) internal pure returns (string memory) {\n        return string(abi.encodePacked(value < 0 ? \"-\" : \"\", toString(SignedMath.abs(value))));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Converts a `uint256` to its ASCII `string` hexadecimal representation.\n     */\n    function toHexString(uint256 value) internal pure returns (string memory) {\n        unchecked {\n            return toHexString(value, Math.log256(value) + 1);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Converts a `uint256` to its ASCII `string` hexadecimal representation with fixed length.\n     */\n    function toHexString(uint256 value, uint256 length) internal pure returns (string memory) {\n        bytes memory buffer = new bytes(2 * length + 2);\n        buffer[0] = \"0\";\n        buffer[1] = \"x\";\n        for (uint256 i = 2 * length + 1; i > 1; --i) {\n            buffer[i] = _SYMBOLS[value & 0xf];\n            value >>= 4;\n        }\n        require(value == 0, \"Strings: hex length insufficient\");\n        return string(buffer);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Converts an `address` with fixed length of 20 bytes to its not checksummed ASCII `string` hexadecimal representation.\n     */\n    function toHexString(address addr) internal pure returns (string memory) {\n        return toHexString(uint256(uint160(addr)), _ADDRESS_LENGTH);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Returns true if the two strings are equal.\n     */\n    function equal(string memory a, string memory b) internal pure returns (bool) {\n        return keccak256(bytes(a)) == keccak256(bytes(b));\n    }\n}\n"

\\_______/   \\___/  |__/ \\______/ |__/  |__/\n  *\n  *  A liquid staking protocol extending Rocket Pool.\n  *  Made w/ <3 by {::}\n  *\n  *  For more information, visit https://nodeset.io\n  *\n  *  @author Mike Leach (Wander), Nick Steinhilber (NickS), Theodore Clapp (mryamz), Joe Clapis (jcrtp), Huy Nguyen, Andy Rose (Barbalute)\n  *  @custom:security-info https://docs.nodeset.io/nodeset/security-notice\n  **/\n\npragma solidity 0.8.17;\n\nimport '@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol';\n\nimport '@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol';\nimport '@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/cryptography/ECDSA.sol';\n\nimport './Utils/UpgradeableBase.sol';\nimport './Utils/Constants.sol';\nimport './OperatorDistributor.sol';\nimport './WETHVault.sol';\nimport './RPLVault.sol';\n\n/// @title MerkleClaimStreamer\n/// @author Mike Leach, Theodore Clapp\n/// @notice Allows claiming of merkle rewards and reports a \"streamed\" value for these assets over a specified time interval to the rest of the \n/// protocol. This prevents the TVL from updating with a significant step which would allow for sandwich attacks.\n/// See this issue with Rocket Pool for a deeper description: https://consensys.io/diligence/audits/2021/04/rocketpool/#rockettokenreth---sandwiching-opportunity-on-price-updates\ncontract MerkleClaimStreamer is UpgradeableBase {\n\n    using Math for uint256;\n\n    event StreamingIntervalChanged(uint256 indexed oldValue, uint256 indexed newValue);\n    event MerkleClaimsEnabledChanged(bool indexed oldValue, bool indexed newValue);\n\n    event MerkleClaimSubmitted(\n        uint256 indexed timestamp, \n        uint256 newEthRewards, \n        uint256 newRplRewards,\n        uint256 ethTreasuryPortion, \n        uint256 ethOperatorPortion, \n        uint256 rplTreasuryPortion\n        );\n\n    // the prior interval's rewards which are \"streamed\" to the TVL \n    uint256 public priorEthStreamAmount; // slot 67\n    uint256 public priorRplStreamAmount; // slot 68\n\n    uint256 public lastClaimTime; // slot 69\n\n    uint256 public streamingInterval;\n\n    // The admin needs to be able to disable merkle claims in case the RP rewards interval is reduced. That way, they can disable claims, then wait for\n    // the current streamingInterval to be completely finished, lower the streamingInterval, and re-enable claims.\n    bool public merkleClaimsEnabled;\n    \n    function initialize(address _directory) public override initializer {\n        super.initialize(_directory);\n        streamingInterval = 28 days; // default RP rewards interval\n        merkleClaimsEnabled = true;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Sets the streaming interval for the MerkleClaimStreamer, which should match the RP rewards interval\n    /// @param _newStreamingInterval The new streaming interval in seconds\n    /// @dev This may also need to be called if there's something wrong with the RP rewards intervals and they are not completed as expected\n    /// if that's the case, the admin should simply change the streaming interval to something else and then immediately back again to trigger\n    /// the sweepLockedTVL function\n    function setStreamingInterval(uint256 _newStreamingInterval) external onlyAdmin {\n        require(_newStreamingInterval > 0 seconds && _newStreamingInterval <= 365 days, \"New streaming interval must be > 0 seconds and <= 365 days\");\n        require(_newStreamingInterval != streamingInterval, \"MerkleClaimStreamer: new streaming interval must be different\");\n        \n        this.sweepLockedTVL();\n\n        emit StreamingIntervalChanged(streamingInterval, _newStreamingInterval);\n        streamingInterval = _newStreamingInterval;\n    }\n\n    function setMerkleClaimsEnabled(bool _isEnabled) external onlyAdmin {\n        require(_isEnabled != merkleClaimsEnabled, \"MerkleClaimStreamer: new merkleClaimsEnabled value must be different\");\n        emit MerkleClaimsEnabledChanged(merkleClaimsEnabled, _isEnabled);\n        merkleClaimsEnabled = _isEnabled;\n    }\n\n    /// @return The current amount of currently-locked TVL which is applicable to xrETH right now\n    function getStreamedTvlEth() public view returns (uint256){\n        uint256 timeSinceLastClaim = block.timestamp - lastClaimTime;\n        return timeSinceLastClaim < streamingInterval ? priorEthStreamAmount * timeSinceLastClaim / streamingInterval : priorEthStreamAmount;\n    }\n\n    /// @return The current amount of currently-locked TVL which is applicable to xRPL right now\n    function getStreamedTvlRpl() public view returns (uint256){\n        uint256 timeSinceLastClaim = block.timestamp - lastClaimTime;\n        return timeSinceLastClaim < streamingInterval ? priorRplStreamAmount * timeSinceLastClaim / streamingInterval : priorRplStreamAmount;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Updates the prior streamed amounts to reflect the current balances\n    /// @dev Called automatically during a merkle claim submission or if the streaming interval is changed\n    function _updatePriorStreamAmounts() internal {\n        priorRplStreamAmount = IERC20(_directory.getRPLAddress()).balanceOf(address(this));\n        priorEthStreamAmount = address(this).balance;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Sweeps the full amount of streamed TVL into the rest of the protocol. Only callable if the full streaming interval has passed.\n    /// @dev Called automatically during a merkle claim submission or if the streaming interval is changed\n    function sweepLockedTVL() public onlyProtocol {\n        require(block.timestamp - lastClaimTime > streamingInterval, \"Current streaming interval is not finished\");\n        if(priorEthStreamAmount == 0 && priorRplStreamAmount == 0) {\n            _updatePriorStreamAmounts();\n            // if both ethAmount and rplAmount are 0, only update streaming amounts\n            // no need to send 0 amounts to the OD or rebalance\n            return; \n        }\n        \n        address payable odAddress = getDirectory().getOperatorDistributorAddress();\n        OperatorDistributor od = OperatorDistributor(odAddress);\n\n        if(priorEthStreamAmount > 0){\n            (bool success, ) = odAddress.call{value: priorEthStreamAmount}(\"\");\n            require(success, \"Failed to transfer ETH from MerkleClaimStreamer to OperatorDistributor\");\n            od.rebalanceWethVault();\n        }\n        \n        if(priorRplStreamAmount > 0){\n            SafeERC20.safeTransfer(IERC20(_directory.getRPLAddress()), odAddress, priorRplStreamAmount);\n            od.rebalanceRplVault();\n        }\n\n        _updatePriorStreamAmounts();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Claims rewards for a node based on a Merkle proof, distributing specified amounts of RPL and ETH.\n     * @dev This function interfaces with the RocketMerkleDistributorMainnet to allow nodes to claim their rewards.\n     *      The rewards are determined by a Merkle proof which validates the amounts to be claimed.\n     * @param rewardIndex Array of indices in the Merkle tree corresponding to reward entries.\n     * @param amountRPL Array of amounts of RPL tokens to claim.\n     * @param amountETH Array of amounts of ETH to claim.\n     * @param merkleProof Array of Merkle proofs for each reward entry.\n     */\n    function submitMerkleClaim(\n        uint256[] calldata rewardIndex,\n        uint256[] calldata amountRPL,\n        uint256[] calldata amountETH,\n        bytes32[][] calldata merkleProof\n    ) public {\n        require(merkleClaimsEnabled, \"Merkle claims are disabled\");\n\n        address payable odAddress = getDirectory().getOperatorDistributorAddress();\n        OperatorDistributor od = OperatorDistributor(odAddress);\n\n        uint256 initialEthBalance = odAddress.balance;\n        uint256 initialRplBalance = IERC20(getDirectory().getRPLAddress()).balanceOf(odAddress);\n        \n        // note: RP will revert if both amountRPL and amountETH are zero\n        od.submitMerkleClaim(rewardIndex, amountRPL, amountETH, merkleProof);\n\n        uint256 ethReward = odAddress.balance - initialEthBalance;\n        uint256 rplReward = IERC20(getDirectory().getRPLAddress()).balanceOf(odAddress) - initialRplBalance;\n\n        // lock all rewards in this contract to be streamed\n        od.transferMerkleClaimToStreamer(ethReward, rplReward);\n\n        uint256 ethTreasuryPortion = 0;\n        uint256 ethOperatorPortion = 0;\n        uint256 rplTreasuryPortion = 0;\n\n        // process ETH fees\n        if(ethReward > 0) {\n            ethTreasuryPortion = WETHVault(getDirectory().getWETHVaultAddress()).getTreasuryPortion(ethReward);\n            ethOperatorPortion = WETHVault(getDirectory().getWETHVaultAddress()).getOperatorPortion(ethReward);\n\n            // send treasury and NO fees out immediately\n            (bool success, ) = getDirectory().getTreasuryAddress().call{value: ethTreasuryPortion}('');\n            require(success, 'Transfer to treasury failed');\n\n            (success, ) = getDirectory().getOperatorRewardAddress().call{value: ethOperatorPortion}('');\n            require(success, 'Transfer to operator reward address failed');\n        }\n        \n        // process RPL fees\n        if(rplReward > 0) {\n            rplTreasuryPortion = RPLVault(getDirectory().getRPLVaultAddress()).getTreasuryPortion(rplReward);\n\n            // send treasury fee immediately\n            SafeERC20.safeTransfer(IERC20(getDirectory().getRPLAddress()), getDirectory().getTreasuryAddress(), rplTreasuryPortion);\n        }\n\n        emit MerkleClaimSubmitted(block.timestamp, ethReward, rplReward, ethTreasuryPortion, ethOperatorPortion, rplTreasuryPortion);\n\n        // sweep all the prior interval's TVL\n        this.sweepLockedTVL();\n\n        lastClaimTime = block.timestamp;\n    }\n\n    // must be payable so OD can send ETH here to be locked up during streaming period\n    receive() external payable {}\n}"

_newValue) external onlyAdmin {\n        require(\n            minipoolProcessingEnabled != _newValue,\n            'OperatorDistributor: new minipoolProcessingEnabled value must be different than existing value'\n        );\n        emit MinipoolProcessingEnabledChanged(_newValue);\n        minipoolProcessingEnabled = _newValue;\n    }\n\n    // Target ratio of SuperNode's bonded ETH to RPL stake.\n    // RPL will be staked if the stake balance is below this and unstaked if the balance is above.\n    uint256 public targetStakeRatio;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Sets the target ratio of the SuperNode's bonded ETH to RPL stake.\n     * @dev RPL will be staked or unstaked to try to reach this ratio during some common state changes.\n     * See rebalanceRplStake()\n     * @param _targetStakeRatio The new target stake ratio to be set.\n     */\n    function setTargetStakeRatio(uint256 _targetStakeRatio) external onlyAdmin {\n        require(\n            _targetStakeRatio != targetStakeRatio,\n            'OperatorDistributor: new targetStakeRatio must be different than existing value'\n        );\n        emit TargetStakeRatioUpdated(targetStakeRatio, _targetStakeRatio);\n        targetStakeRatio = _targetStakeRatio;\n    }\n\n    // Minimum ratio of matched rETH to RPL stake allowed in the node.\n    uint256 public minimumStakeRatio;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Sets the minimum ratio of matched rETH to RPL stake allowed in the node.\n     * @dev minipools can't be created if the new stake ratio would be below this amount.\n     * @param _minimumStakeRatio The new minimum stake ratio to be set.\n     */\n    function setMinimumStakeRatio(uint256 _minimumStakeRatio) external onlyAdmin {\n        require(\n            _minimumStakeRatio != minimumStakeRatio,\n            'OperatorDistributor: new minimumStakeRatio must be different than existing value'\n        );\n        emit MinStakeRatioUpdated(minimumStakeRatio, _minimumStakeRatio);\n        minimumStakeRatio = _minimumStakeRatio;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Initializes the contract with the provided storage address.\n     * @dev This function should only be called once, during contract creation or proxy initialization.\n     * It overrides the `initialize` function from a parent contract.\n     * @param _directory The address of the storage contract.\n     */\n    function initialize(address _directory) public override initializer {\n        super.initialize(_directory);\n        targetStakeRatio = 0.6e18; // 60% of bonded ETH by default.\n        minimumStakeRatio = 0.15e18; // 15% of matched ETH by default\n        minipoolProcessingEnabled = true;\n        rplStakeRebalanceEnabled = true;\n    }\n\n    // Index for the current minipool being processed\n    uint256 public lastProcessedMinipoolIndex;\n\n    // The amount the oracle has already included in its summation\n    // This is important to track because when a minipool is skimmed, its balance will have\n    // been reported already by the oracle, so there will be an extra amount of ETH TVL reported\n    // otherwise\n    uint256 public oracleError;\n\n    /**\n     * VIEWS\n     */\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Returns the total ETH and WETH managed by the contract, including both the ETH+WETH balances of this contract\n     * and the SuperNode's staked ETH.\n     * @return uint256 Total amount of ETH under the management of the contract.\n     */\n    function getTvlEth() public view returns (uint) {\n        return\n            address(this).balance +\n            IWETH(_directory.getWETHAddress()).balanceOf(address(this)) +\n            SuperNodeAccount(_directory.getSuperNodeAddress()).getEthStaked();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Returns the total RPL managed by the contract, including both the balance of this contract\n     * and the SuperNode's staked RPL.\n     * @return uint256 Total amount of RPL under the management of the contract.\n     */\n    function getTvlRpl() public view returns (uint) {\n        return\n            IERC20(_directory.getRPLAddress()).balanceOf(address(this)) +\n            SuperNodeAccount(_directory.getSuperNodeAddress()).getRplStaked();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculates the additional RPL needed to maintain the minimum staking ratio.\n     * @param _existingRplStake Current amount of RPL staked by the node.\n     * @param _rpEthMatched Amount of rETH currently matched with the node.\n     * @return requiredStakeRpl Amount of additional RPL needed to reach the minimumStakeRatio.\n     */\n    function calculateRplStakeShortfall(\n        uint256 _existingRplStake,\n        uint256 _rpEthMatched\n    ) public view returns (uint256 requiredStakeRpl) {\n        uint256 ethPriceInRpl = PriceFetcher(getDirectory().getPriceFetcherAddress()).getPrice();\n        uint256 matchedStakeRatio = _rpEthMatched == 0 || ethPriceInRpl == 0\n            ? 0\n            : (_existingRplStake * 1e18 * 1e18) / (_rpEthMatched * ethPriceInRpl);\n        if (matchedStakeRatio < minimumStakeRatio) {\n            uint256 minRplStake = minimumStakeRatio.mulDiv(_rpEthMatched * ethPriceInRpl, 1e18 * 1e18);\n            requiredStakeRpl = minRplStake < _existingRplStake ? 0 : minRplStake - _existingRplStake;\n        } else {\n            requiredStakeRpl = 0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculates the maximum RPL that can be withdrawn while maintaining the target staking ratio.\n     * @dev Used for tests\n     * @param _existingRplStake Current amount of RPL staked by the node.\n     * @param _ethStaked Amount of ETH currently staked by the node.\n     * @return withdrawableStakeRpl Maximum RPL that can be safely withdrawn.\n     */\n    function calculateRequiredRplTopDown(\n        uint256 _existingRplStake,\n        uint256 _ethStaked\n    ) public view returns (uint256 withdrawableStakeRpl) {\n        uint256 ethPriceInRpl = PriceFetcher(getDirectory().getPriceFetcherAddress()).getPrice();\n\n        uint256 stakeRatio = _existingRplStake == 0 ? 1e18 : (_ethStaked * ethPriceInRpl * 1e18) / _existingRplStake;\n        if (stakeRatio > targetStakeRatio) {\n            uint256 maxRplStake = targetStakeRatio.mulDiv(_ethStaked * ethPriceInRpl, 1e18 * 1e18);\n            withdrawableStakeRpl = _existingRplStake > maxRplStake ? _existingRplStake - maxRplStake : 0;\n        } else {\n            withdrawableStakeRpl = 0;\n        }\n        return withdrawableStakeRpl;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Note for the following functions: Ideally, minipools would be processed in order of the longest since last processing,\n     * but there are some scenarios where minipools may be processed out of order for the sake of gas efficiency with other operations.\n     * For example:\n     * - when minipools are removed (which swaps the last minipool with the removed one in the list)\n     * - when minipools are created (which adds them to the end of the list)\n     */\n\n    /**\n     * @dev This function helps read state for the rotation and handling of different minipools within the system.\n     * @return IMinipool Returns the next minipool to process. Returns a binding to the zero address if there are no minipools.\n     */\n    function getNextMinipool() public view returns (IMinipool) {\n        SuperNodeAccount sna = SuperNodeAccount(getDirectory().getSuperNodeAddress());\n        if (sna.getNumMinipools() == 0) {\n            return IMinipool(address(0));\n        }\n        return IMinipool(sna.minipools(this.getNextMinipoolIndex()));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev This function helps read state for the rotation and handling of different minipools within the system.\n     * @return Returns the next minipool index which will be processed or 0 if there are no minipools\n     */\n    function getNextMinipoolIndex() public view returns (uint256) {\n        SuperNodeAccount sna = SuperNodeAccount(getDirectory().getSuperNodeAddress());\n        if (sna.getNumMinipools() == 0) {\n            return 0;\n        }\n        return\n            lastProcessedMinipoolIndex + 1 < SuperNodeAccount(getDirectory().getSuperNodeAddress()).getNumMinipools()\n                ? lastProcessedMinipoolIndex + 1\n                : 0;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * PUBLIC ACTIONS\n     */\n\n    receive() external payable {}\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Process the next minipool in line, then increments lastProcessedMinipoolIndex.\n     * Handles RPL rebalancing and minipool distribution based on minipool's current state.\n     * Although this can be called manually, this typically happens automatically as part of other state changes\n     * like claiming NO fees or depositing/withdrawing from the token vaults.\n     * See processMinipool() for more info (this is a very important function).\n     */\n    function processNextMinipool() public {\n        IMinipool nextMinipool = getNextMinipool();\n        if (address(nextMinipool) == address(0)) {\n            // Nothing to do\n            return;\n        }\n        processMinipool(nextMinipool);\n        lastProcessedMinipoolIndex = this.getNextMinipoolIndex();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @custom:author Mike Leach (Wander)\n     * @notice This is the \"tick\" function of the protocol. It's the heartbeat of Constellation,\n     * and it's called every time deposits and withdrawals from the xrETH and xRPL vaults\n     * @dev Performs a RPL stake rebalance for the node, distributes the outstanding balance for a minipool,\n     * and then rebalance protocol liquidity.\n     */\n    function processMinipool(IMinipool minipool) public {\n        if (!minipoolProcessingEnabled) return; // emergency switch for minipool processing\n        if (address(minipool) == address(0)) {\n            return; // should only happen if there are no minipools in the system\n        }\n        if (address(minipool).balance == 0) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        SuperNodeAccount sna = SuperNodeAccount(_directory.getSuperNodeAddress());\n        require(sna.getIsMinipoolRecognized(address(minipool)), 'Must be a minipool managed by Constellation');\n\n        MinipoolStatus status = minipool.getStatus();\n\n        if (minipool.getFinalised() || status != MinipoolStatus.Staking) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // get refs and data\n        (, uint256 treasuryFee, uint256 noFee, ) = sna.minipoolData(address(minipool));\n\n        // determine the difference in node share and remaining bond amount\n        uint256 depositBalance = minipool.getNodeDepositBalance();\n        // if nodeshare - original deposit is <= 0 then it's an exit but there are no rewards (it's been penalized)\n        uint256 rewards = 0;\n\n        // In Constellation, the node refund balance is assumed to be 100% rewards, even though this isn't always true in RP.\n        // There are three cases where this can happen, but none of these should be possible in Constellation:\n        // - LEB bond reductions\n        // - depositType == MinipoolDeposit.Full\n        // - prepareVacancy() -- used for solo node migrations to RP\n        uint256 balanceAfterRefund = address(minipool).balance - minipool.getNodeRefundBalance();\n\n        if (balanceAfterRefund >= depositBalance) {\n            // it's an exit\n            // In case there is a penalty, just return so it can be handled manually.\n            // This prevents the case where someone sends 8 ETH to the minipool and it's automatically closed,\n            // causing RP to think it's been slashed for 24 ETH even though the validator is still operating\n            if (\n                address(minipool).balance <\n                IRocketDAOProtocolSettingsMinipool(getDirectory().getRocketDAOProtocolSettingsMinipool())\n                    .getLaunchBalance()\n            ) {\n                emit SuspectedPenalizedMinipoolExit(address(minipool));\n                return;\n            }\n\n            this.distributeExitedMinipool(minipool);\n        } else if (balanceAfterRefund < depositBalance) {\n            // it's still staking\n            uint256 priorBalance = address(this).balance;\n            // withdrawal address calls distributeBalance(true)\n            minipool.distributeBalance(true);\n            // calculate rewards\n            rewards = address(this).balance > priorBalance ? address(this).balance - priorBalance : 0;\n            this.onEthBeaconRewardsReceived(rewards, treasuryFee, noFee);\n        }\n\n        this.rebalanceWethVault();\n        this.rebalanceRplVault();\n        this.rebalanceRplStake(sna.getEthStaked());\n\n        emit MinipoolProcessed(address(minipool), rewards, minipool.getFinalised());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * INTERNAL\n     */\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Adjusts the RPL stake of the SuperNode to maintain the target RPL stake ratio based on the current ETH price\n     *  and staking metrics.\n     * @dev Internal only. Called during processMinipool() or minipool creation.\n     * @param _ethStaked Amount of ETH currently staked in the SuperNode.\n     */\n    function rebalanceRplStake(uint256 _ethStaked) public onlyProtocol {\n        if (!rplStakeRebalanceEnabled) return;\n        address _nodeAccount = _directory.getSuperNodeAddress();\n\n        IRocketNodeStaking rocketNodeStaking = IRocketNodeStaking(_directory.getRocketNodeStakingAddress());\n        uint256 rplStaked = rocketNodeStaking.getNodeRPLStake(_nodeAccount);\n        uint256 lockedStake = rocketNodeStaking.getNodeRPLLocked(_nodeAccount);\n\n        uint256 ethPriceInRpl = PriceFetcher(getDirectory().getPriceFetcherAddress()).getPrice();\n\n        uint256 targetStake = targetStakeRatio.mulDiv(_ethStaked * ethPriceInRpl, 1e18 * 10 ** 18);\n\n        // need to stake more\n        if (targetStake > rplStaked) {\n            uint256 stakeIncrease = targetStake - rplStaked;\n\n            uint256 currentRplBalance = IERC20(_directory.getRPLAddress()).balanceOf(address(this));\n\n            if (currentRplBalance >= stakeIncrease) {\n                this.stakeRpl(stakeIncrease);\n            } else {\n                // stake what we have\n                if (currentRplBalance == 0) return;\n                this.stakeRpl(currentRplBalance);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // need to unstake\n        if (targetStake < rplStaked) {\n            uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp -\n                IRocketNodeStaking(_directory.getRocketNodeStakingAddress()).getNodeRPLStakedTime(_nodeAccount);\n\n            uint256 excessRpl = rplStaked - targetStake;\n            bool noShortfall = rplStaked - excessRpl - lockedStake >=\n                rocketNodeStaking.getNodeMaximumRPLStake(_nodeAccount);\n            if (\n                elapsed >=\n                IRocketDAOProtocolSettingsRewards(_directory.getRocketDAOProtocolSettingsRewardsAddress())\n                    .getRewardsClaimIntervalTime() &&\n                noShortfall\n            ) {\n                this.unstakeRpl(excessRpl);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Unstakes a specified amount of RPL tokens.\n    /// @dev This function unstakes a specified amount of RPL tokens from the Rocket Node Staking contract.\n    /// @param _amount The amount of RPL tokens to unstake.\n    /// @dev The tokens will be withdrawn from the Rocket Node Staking contract into this contract.\n    /// Outside callers MUST call onRplBalanceIncrease or onRplBalanceDecrease to appropriately account for this.\n    function unstakeRpl(uint256 _amount) external onlyProtocol {\n        IRocketNodeStaking(getDirectory().getRocketNodeStakingAddress()).withdrawRPL(\n            getDirectory().getSuperNodeAddress(),\n            _amount\n        );\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Stakes a specified amount of RPL tokens on behalf of the SuperNode.\n    /// @dev This function allows the protocol to stake a specified amount of RPL tokens on the SuperNode\n    ///      using the Rocket Node Staking contract.\n    /// @param _amount The amount of RPL tokens to stake.\n    /// @dev This function ensures that the specified amount of RPL tokens is approved and then staked\n    /// for the SuperNode.\n    function stakeRpl(uint256 _amount) external onlyProtocol {\n        IERC20 rpl = IERC20(_directory.getRPLAddress());\n        address rocketNodeStakingAddress = _directory.getRocketNodeStakingAddress();\n        SafeERC20.safeApprove(rpl, rocketNodeStakingAddress, 0);\n        SafeERC20.safeApprove(rpl, rocketNodeStakingAddress, _amount);\n        IRocketNodeStaking(rocketNodeStakingAddress).stakeRPLFor(getDirectory().getSuperNodeAddress(), _amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Submits a merkle claim to RP on behalf of the SuperNode\n    /// @dev Although all other merkle logic is in the MerkleClaimStreamer, a merkle claim must be done by the node or withdrawal address\n    function submitMerkleClaim(\n        uint256[] calldata rewardIndex,\n        uint256[] calldata amountRPL,\n        uint256[] calldata amountETH,\n        bytes32[][] calldata merkleProof\n    ) external onlyProtocol {\n        IRocketMerkleDistributorMainnet(_directory.getRocketMerkleDistributorMainnetAddress()).claim(\n            address(getDirectory().getSuperNodeAddress()),\n            rewardIndex,\n            amountRPL,\n            amountETH,\n            merkleProof\n        );\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Distributes ETH among the vault and operator distributor.\n    /// @dev Transfers any required WETH liquidity to the vault and retains the surplus here as ETH in the operator distributor.\n    function rebalanceWethVault() public onlyProtocol {\n        IWETH weth = IWETH(_directory.getWETHAddress());\n\n        // Initialize the vault and operator distributor addresses\n        WETHVault vweth = WETHVault(getDirectory().getWETHVaultAddress());\n\n        uint256 requiredWeth = vweth.getMissingLiquidity();\n        uint256 wethBalance = IERC20(address(weth)).balanceOf(address(this));\n        uint256 balanceEthAndWeth = wethBalance + address(this).balance;\n        if (balanceEthAndWeth >= requiredWeth) {\n            // there's extra ETH that can be kept here for minipools, so only send required amount\n            // figure out how much to wrap, then wrap it\n            uint256 wrapNeeded = requiredWeth > wethBalance ? requiredWeth - wethBalance : 0;\n            if (wrapNeeded != 0) weth.deposit{value: wrapNeeded}();\n            // send amount needed to vault\n            if (requiredWeth != 0) SafeERC20.safeTransfer(weth, address(vweth), requiredWeth);\n            // unwrap the remaining balance to keep for minipool creation (technically not necessary, but done in case WETH is sent to this contract)\n            weth.withdraw(IERC20(address(weth)).balanceOf(address(this)));\n        } else {\n            // not enough available to fill up the liquidity reserve, so send everything we can\n            // wrap everything in this contract and give back to the WethVault for liquidity\n            weth.deposit{value: address(this).balance}();\n            // note: must recheck weth balance because it will change in the above line\n            SafeERC20.safeTransfer(IERC20(address(weth)), address(vweth), weth.balanceOf(address(this)));\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Rebalances RPL among the vault and operator distributor.\n    /// @dev Transfers any required RPL liquidity to the vault and retains the surplus here in the operator distributor.\n    function rebalanceRplVault() public onlyProtocol {\n        // Initialize the RPLVault and the Operator Distributor addresses\n        RPLVault vrpl = RPLVault(getDirectory().getRPLVaultAddress());\n\n        IERC20 rpl = IERC20(_directory.getRPLAddress());\n        uint256 rplBalance = rpl.balanceOf(address(this));\n\n        // Fetch the required capital in RPL and the total RPL balance of the contract\n        uint256 requiredRpl = vrpl.getMissingLiquidity();\n\n        // Transfer RPL to the RPLVault\n        if (rplBalance >= requiredRpl) {\n            // there's extra that can be kept here for minipools, so only send required amount\n            if (requiredRpl != 0) SafeERC20.safeTransfer(IERC20(address(rpl)), address(vrpl), requiredRpl);\n        } else {\n            // not enough here to fill up the liquidity reserve, so send everything we can\n            SafeERC20.safeTransfer(IERC20(address(rpl)), address(vrpl), rplBalance);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Called by the protocol when a minipool is distributed to this contract, which acts as the SuperNode\n    /// withdrawal address for both ETH and RPL from Rocket Pool.\n    /// Splits incoming assets up among the Treasury, YieldDistributor, and the WETHVault/OperatorDistributor based on the\n    /// rewardsAmount expected.\n    /// @param rewardAmount amount of ETH rewards expected\n    /// @param avgTreasuryFee Average treasury fee for the rewards received across all the minipools the rewards came from\n    /// @param avgOperatorsFee Average operator fee for the rewards received across all the minipools the rewards came from\n    function onEthBeaconRewardsReceived(\n        uint256 rewardAmount,\n        uint256 avgTreasuryFee,\n        uint256 avgOperatorsFee\n    ) public onlyProtocol {\n        if (rewardAmount == 0) return;\n\n        uint256 treasuryPortion = rewardAmount.mulDiv(avgTreasuryFee, 1e18);\n        uint256 nodeOperatorPortion = rewardAmount.mulDiv(avgOperatorsFee, 1e18);\n\n        (bool success, ) = getDirectory().getTreasuryAddress().call{value: treasuryPortion}('');\n        require(success, 'Transfer to treasury failed');\n\n        (bool success2, ) = getDirectory().getOperatorRewardAddress().call{value: nodeOperatorPortion}('');\n        require(success2, 'Transfer to operator fee address failed');\n\n        uint256 xrETHPortion = rewardAmount - treasuryPortion - nodeOperatorPortion;\n\n        emit EthBeaconRewardsReceived(rewardAmount, nodeOperatorPortion, treasuryPortion);\n        this.onIncreaseOracleError(xrETHPortion);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Finalizes and distributes the balance of an exited minipool.\n    /// @dev Callable by the admin in case there is a need for manual finalization of an exited minipool.\n    /// This should only happen if the minipool was slashed or otherwise penalized so the balance is below 32 ETH upon exit.\n    /// Otherwise, the processNextMinipool() function would finalize the minipool normally with this function.\n    function distributeExitedMinipool(IMinipool minipool) public onlyProtocolOrAdmin {\n        SuperNodeAccount sna = SuperNodeAccount(getDirectory().getSuperNodeAddress());\n\n        if (address(minipool).balance < minipool.getNodeRefundBalance()) {\n            emit WarningEthBalanceSmallerThanRefundBalance(address(minipool));\n            return;\n        }\n\n        uint256 originalBalance = address(this).balance;\n        minipool.distributeBalance(false);\n        // if the amount received is more than the original bond, it's rewards; otherwise no rewards\n        uint256 rewards = address(this).balance - originalBalance > minipool.getNodeDepositBalance()\n            ? address(this).balance - originalBalance - minipool.getNodeDepositBalance()\n            : 0;\n\n        // stop tracking\n        (address operatorAddress, uint256 treasuryFee, uint256 noFee, ) = sna.minipoolData(address(minipool));\n        Whitelist(getDirectory().getWhitelistAddress()).removeValidator(operatorAddress);\n        sna.removeMinipool(address(minipool));\n        // account for rewards\n        this.onEthBeaconRewardsReceived(rewards, treasuryFee, noFee);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Allocates the necessary liquidity for the creation of a new minipool.\n     */\n    function sendEthForMinipool() external onlyProtocol {\n        address payable snaAddress = getDirectory().getSuperNodeAddress();\n        uint256 bond = SuperNodeAccount(snaAddress).bond();\n\n        (bool success, bytes memory data) = snaAddress.call{value: bond}('');\n        if (!success) {\n            revert LowLevelEthTransfer(success, data);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Resets the oracle error.\n     */\n    function resetOracleError() external onlyProtocol {\n        oracleError = 0;\n    }\n\n    function onIncreaseOracleError(uint256 _amount) external onlyProtocol {\n        oracleError += _amount;\n    }\n\n    function transferMerkleClaimToStreamer(uint256 ethAmount, uint256 rplAmount) external onlyProtocol {\n        address payable mcsAddress = getDirectory().getMerkleClaimStreamerAddress();\n\n        if (ethAmount > 0) {\n            (bool success, ) = mcsAddress.call{value: ethAmount}('');\n            require(success, 'ETH transfer to MerkleClaimStreamer failed');\n        }\n\n        if (rplAmount > 0) {\n            SafeERC20.safeTransfer(IERC20(_directory.getRPLAddress()), mcsAddress, rplAmount);\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
    },

** |____  **|_  **_/  | ** /**__  **| **__  **\n  * |  **           | **       | **      | **  \\ **| **  \\ **|  ******   | **    | ********| **| **  /*******  | **    | **| **  \\ **| **  \\ **\n  *  \\ **   /** /** | **       | **    **| **  | **| **  | ** \\____  **  | ** /* | **_____/| **| ** /**__  **  | ** /* | **| **  | **| **  | **\n  *  |  ***|__/|__/***         |  ******||  ****** | **  | ** /*******   | ****  |  *******| **| **| ********  | ****  | **|  ****** | **  | **\n  *   \\___/       |___/         \\______/  \\______/ |__/  |__/|_______/    \\___/   \\_______/|__/|__/ \\_______/   \\___/  |__/ \\______/ |__/  |__/\n  *\n  *  A liquid staking protocol extending Rocket Pool.\n  *  Made w/ <3 by {::}\n  *\n  *  For more information, visit https://nodeset.io\n  *\n  *  @author Mike Leach (Wander), Nick Steinhilber (NickS), Theodore Clapp (mryamz), Joe Clapis (jcrtp), Huy Nguyen, Andy Rose (Barbalute)\n  *  @custom:security-info https://docs.nodeset.io/nodeset/security-notice\n  **/\n\npragma solidity 0.8.17;\n\nimport '@openzeppelin/contracts-upgradeable/token/ERC20/extensions/ERC4626Upgradeable.sol';\nimport '@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol';\nimport '@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol';\n\nimport './WETHVault.sol';\nimport './SuperNodeAccount.sol';\nimport './MerkleClaimStreamer.sol';\n\nimport './Utils/UpgradeableBase.sol';\nimport './OperatorDistributor.sol';\n\nimport './Utils/PriceFetcher.sol';\nimport '../Interfaces/IRateProvider.sol';\n\n/**\n * @title RPLVault\n * @author Theodore Clapp, Mike Leach\n * @dev An ERC-4626 vault for staking RPL with a single node run by a decentralized operator set.\n * @notice These vault shares will increase or decrease in value according to the rewards or penalties applied to the SuperNodeAccount by Rocket Pool.\n */\ncontract RPLVault is UpgradeableBase, ERC4626Upgradeable, IRateProvider {\n    using Math for uint256;\n\n    event TreasuryFeeChanged(uint256 indexed oldFee, uint256 indexed newFee);\n    event MinWethRplRatioChanged(uint256 indexed oldValue, uint256 indexed newValue);\n    event RPLLiquidityReservePercentChanged(uint256 indexed oldValue, uint256 indexed newValue);\n    event DifferingSenderRecipientEnabledChanged(bool indexed oldValue, bool indexed newValue);\n\n    string constant NAME = 'Constellation RPL';\n    string constant SYMBOL = 'xRPL';\n\n    uint256 public treasuryFee;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Sets the liquidity reserve as a percentage of TVL. E.g. if set to 2% (0.02e18), then 2% of the \n     * RPL backing xRPL will be reserved for withdrawals. If the reserve is below maximum, it will be refilled before assets are\n     * put to work with the OperatorDistributor.\n     */\n    uint256 public liquidityReservePercent;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice the minimum percentage of ETH/RPL TVL allowed \n     * @dev this is a simple percentage, because the RPL TVL is calculated using its price in ETH\n     */\n    uint256 public minWethRplRatio;\n\n    bool public depositsEnabled;\n\n    // can the recipient of a deposit be different than the caller? False by default for extra security\n    bool public differingSenderRecipientEnabled;\n    \n    /**\n     * @notice Initializes the vault with necessary parameters and settings.\n     * @dev This function sets up the vault's token references, fee structures, and various configurations. It's intended to be called once after deployment.\n     * @param directoryAddress Address of the directory contract to reference other platform contracts.\n     * @param rplToken Address of the RPL token contract to be used in this vault.\n     */\n    function initializeVault(address directoryAddress, address rplToken) public virtual initializer {\n        super.initialize(directoryAddress);\n        ERC4626Upgradeable.__ERC4626_init(IERC20Upgradeable(rplToken));\n        ERC20Upgradeable.__ERC20_init(NAME, SYMBOL);\n\n        liquidityReservePercent = 0.02e18;\n        minWethRplRatio = 0; // 0% by default\n        treasuryFee = 0.01e18;\n        depositsEnabled = true;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Handles deposits into the vault, ensuring compliance with WETH coverage ratio.\n     * @dev This function first checks if the WETH coverage ratio after deposit will still be above the threshold, \n     * and then continues with the deposit process. The deposited amount is transferred to the OperatorDistributor for utilization.\n     * This function overrides the `_deposit` function in the parent contract to ensure custom business logic is applied.\n     * Processes a minipool after depositing, then rebalances the RPL liquidity.\n     * @param caller The address initiating the deposit.\n     * @param receiver The address designated to receive the issued shares for the deposit.\n     * @param assets The amount of assets being deposited.\n     * @param shares The number of shares to be exchanged for the deposit.\n     */\n    function _deposit(address caller, address receiver, uint256 assets, uint256 shares) internal virtual override {\n        require(depositsEnabled, \"deposits are disabled\"); // emergency switch for deposits\n        require(differingSenderRecipientEnabled || caller == receiver, 'caller must be receiver');\n        require(!_directory.isSanctioned(caller, receiver), \"RPLVault: cannot deposit from or to a sanctioned address\");\n        WETHVault vweth = WETHVault(_directory.getWETHVaultAddress());\n\n        require(vweth.tvlRatioEthRpl(assets, false) >= minWethRplRatio, 'insufficient weth coverage ratio');\n\n        OperatorDistributor od = OperatorDistributor(_directory.getOperatorDistributorAddress());\n\n        super._deposit(caller, receiver, assets, shares);\n\n        SafeERC20.safeTransfer(IERC20(asset()), address(od), IERC20(asset()).balanceOf(address(this)));\n\n        od.processNextMinipool();\n        od.rebalanceRplVault();  // just in case there are no minipools to process, rebalance anyway\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Handles withdrawals from the vault.\n     * @dev This function overrides the `_withdraw` function in the parent contract to\n     * ensure custom business logic is applied. May revert if the liquidity reserves are too low.\n     * Processes a minipool before withdrawing, then rebalances the RPL liquidity.\n     * @param caller The address initiating the withdrawal.\n     * @param receiver The address designated to receive the withdrawn assets.\n     * @param owner The address that owns the shares being redeemed.\n     * @param assets The amount of assets being withdrawn.\n     * @param shares The number of shares to be burned in exchange for the withdrawal.\n     */ function _withdraw(\n        address caller,\n        address receiver,\n        address owner,\n        uint256 assets,\n        uint256 shares\n    ) internal virtual override {\n        require(caller == receiver, 'caller must be receiver');\n        \n        OperatorDistributor od = OperatorDistributor(_directory.getOperatorDistributorAddress());\n        // first process a minipool to give the best chance at actually withdrawing\n        od.processNextMinipool();\n\n        require(IERC20(asset()).balanceOf(address(this)) >= assets, 'Not enough liquidity to withdraw');\n\n        super._withdraw(caller, receiver, owner, assets, shares);\n        \n        od.rebalanceRplVault(); // just in case there are no minipools to process, rebalance anyway\n    }\n\n    function _transfer(\n        address sender,\n        address recipient,\n        uint256 shares\n    ) internal virtual override {\n        require(!_directory.isSanctioned(sender, recipient), \"RPLVault: transfer not allowed from or to sanctioned address\");\n        super._transfer(sender, recipient, shares);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Returns the total assets managed by this vault. That is, all the RPL backing xRPL.\n     * @return The aggregated total assets managed by this vault.\n     */\n    function totalAssets() public view override returns (uint256) {\n        return (IERC20(asset()).balanceOf(address(this)) +\n            OperatorDistributor(_directory.getOperatorDistributorAddress()).getTvlRpl()) +\n            MerkleClaimStreamer(getDirectory().getMerkleClaimStreamerAddress()).getStreamedTvlRpl();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculates the missing liquidity needed to meet the liquidity reserve after a specified deposit.\n     * @dev Compares the current balance with the required liquidity based on the total assets including the deposit and mint fee.\n     * @param deposit The amount of the new deposit to consider in the liquidity calculation.\n     * @return The amount of liquidity required after the specified deposit.\n     */\n    function getMissingLiquidityAfterDeposit(uint256 deposit) public view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 currentBalance = IERC20(asset()).balanceOf(address(this));\n        uint256 fullBalance = totalAssets() + deposit;\n        uint256 requiredBalance = liquidityReservePercent.mulDiv(fullBalance, 1e18, Math.Rounding.Up);\n        return requiredBalance > currentBalance ? requiredBalance - currentBalance: 0;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculates the missing liquidity needed to meet the liquidity reserve.\n     * @dev This function calculates the current assets needed to hit the liquidity reserve \n     * based the current total assets of the vault.\n     * @return The amount of liquidity required.\n     */\n    function getMissingLiquidity() public view returns (uint256) {\n        return getMissingLiquidityAfterDeposit(0);\n    }\n\n    /// Calculates the treasury portion of a specific RPL reward amount.\n    /// @param _amount The RPL reward expected\n    function getTreasuryPortion(uint256 _amount) external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _amount.mulDiv(treasuryFee, 1e18);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Convenience function for determining if a particular deposit amount will be allowed or rejected\n     */\n    function getIsDepositAllowed(uint256 amount) public view returns (bool) {\n        return WETHVault(_directory.getWETHVaultAddress()).tvlRatioEthRpl(amount, false) >= minWethRplRatio;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Convenience function for determining if a particular withdraw amount will be allowed or rejected\n     */\n    function getIsWithdrawAllowed(uint256 amount) public view returns (bool) {\n        return IERC20(asset()).balanceOf(address(this)) >= amount;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Convenience function for viewing the maximum withdrawal allowed\n     */\n    function getMaximumWithdrawAmount() public view returns (uint256) {\n        return IERC20(asset()).balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Convenience function for viewing the maximum deposit allowed\n     */\n    function getMaximumDeposit() public view returns (uint256) {\n        if(minWethRplRatio == 0) return type(uint256).max;\n        WETHVault wethVault = WETHVault(_directory.getWETHVaultAddress());\n        if(wethVault.tvlRatioEthRpl(0, false) < minWethRplRatio) return 0;\n\n        uint256 tvlRpl = totalAssets();\n        uint256 tvlEth = WETHVault(getDirectory().getWETHVaultAddress()).totalAssets();\n        uint256 rplPerEth = PriceFetcher(getDirectory().getPriceFetcherAddress()).getPrice();\n\n        return ((tvlEth * rplPerEth) / minWethRplRatio) - tvlRpl;\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Shortcut for easier defi integration (e.g. Balancer)\n    /// @return The value of 1 xRPL in terms of RPL\n    function getRate() public view returns (uint256) {\n        return convertToAssets(1 ether);\n    }\n\n    /**ADMIN FUNCTIONS */\n\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Sets whether the recipient of a deposit is allowed to be different than the caller of the deposit function.\n     * @param _newValue The new value for the differingSenderRecipientEnabled variable\n     */\n    function setDifferingSenderRecipientEnabled(bool _newValue) external onlyAdmin {\n        require(_newValue != differingSenderRecipientEnabled, 'RPLVault: new differingSenderRecipientEnabled value must be different than existing value');\n        emit DifferingSenderRecipientEnabledChanged(differingSenderRecipientEnabled, _newValue);\n        differingSenderRecipientEnabled = _newValue;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Sets the treasury fee basis points.\n     * @dev This function allows the admin to update the fee basis points that the treasury will receive from the rewards.\n     * The treasury fee must be less than or equal to 100% (1e18 basis points).\n     * @param _treasuryFee The new treasury fee in basis points.\n     * @custom:requires This function can only be called by an address with the Medium Timelock role.\n     */\n    function setTreasuryFee(uint256 _treasuryFee) external onlyMediumTimelock {\n        require(_treasuryFee <= 1e18, 'Fee too high');\n        require(_treasuryFee != treasuryFee, 'RPLVault: new treasury fee value must be different than existing value');\n        emit TreasuryFeeChanged(treasuryFee, _treasuryFee);\n        treasuryFee = _treasuryFee;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Update the WETH coverage ratio.\n     * @dev This function allows the admin to adjust the WETH coverage ratio.\n     * The ratio determines the minimum coverage required to ensure the contract's health and stability.\n     * It's expressed in base points, where 1e18 represents 100%.\n     * @param _minWethRplRatio The new WETH coverage ratio to be set (in base points).\n     */\n    function setMinWethRplRatio(uint256 _minWethRplRatio) external onlyShortTimelock {\n        require(_minWethRplRatio != minWethRplRatio, 'RPLVault: new minWethRplRatio value must be different than existing value');\n        emit MinWethRplRatioChanged(minWethRplRatio, _minWethRplRatio);\n        minWethRplRatio = _minWethRplRatio;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Sets the liquidity reserve as a percentage of TVL. E.g. if set to 2% (0.02e18), then 2% of the \n     * RPL backing xRPL will be reserved for withdrawals. If the reserve is below maximum, it will be refilled before assets are\n     * put to work with the OperatorDistributor.\n     * @dev This function allows the admin to update the liquidity reserve which determines the amount available for withdrawals.\n     * The liquidity reserve must be a reasonable percentage between 0 and 100%. 1e18 = 100%\n     * @param _liquidityReservePercent The new collateralization ratio in basis points.\n     * @custom:requires This function can only be called by an address with the Medium Timelock role.\n     */\n    function setLiquidityReservePercent(uint256 _liquidityReservePercent) external onlyShortTimelock {\n        require(_liquidityReservePercent <= 1e18, 'RPLVault: liquidity reserve percentage must be less than or equal to 100%');\n        require(_liquidityReservePercent != liquidityReservePercent, 'RPLVault: new liquidityReservePercent value must be different than existing value');\n\n        emit RPLLiquidityReservePercentChanged(liquidityReservePercent, _liquidityReservePercent);\n\n        liquidityReservePercent = _liquidityReservePercent;\n\n        // rebalance entire balance of the contract to ensure the new liquidity reserve is respected\n        OperatorDistributor od = OperatorDistributor(_directory.getOperatorDistributorAddress());\n        SafeERC20.safeTransfer(IERC20(asset()), address(od), IERC20(asset()).balanceOf(address(this)));\n        od.rebalanceRplVault();\n        od.rebalanceRplStake(SuperNodeAccount(getDirectory().getSuperNodeAddress()).getEthStaked());\n    }\n\n    function setDepositsEnabled(bool _newValue) external onlyAdmin {\n        depositsEnabled = _newValue;\n    }\n}\n"
    },
