staking protocol extending Rocket Pool.\n *  Made w/ <3 by {::}\n *\n *  For more information, visit https://nodeset.io\n *\n *  @author Mike Leach (Wander), Nick Steinhilber (NickS), Theodore Clapp (mryamz), Joe Clapis (jcrtp), Huy Nguyen, Andy Rose (Barbalute)\n *  @custom:security-info https://docs.nodeset.io/nodeset/security-notice\n **/\n\npragma solidity 0.8.17;\n\nimport '@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol';\nimport '@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/cryptography/ECDSA.sol';\n\nimport './OperatorDistributor.sol';\n\nimport './Whitelist.sol';\nimport './Utils/UpgradeableBase.sol';\n\nimport '../Interfaces/RocketPool/IRocketNodeDeposit.sol';\nimport '../Interfaces/RocketPool/IRocketNodeStaking.sol';\nimport '../Interfaces/RocketPool/IRocketNodeManager.sol';\nimport '../Interfaces/RocketPool/IRocketMinipoolManager.sol';\n\nimport '../Interfaces/RocketPool/IRocketMerkleDistributorMainnet.sol';\nimport '../Interfaces/RocketPool/IRocketDAOProtocolSettingsMinipool.sol';\nimport '../Interfaces/RocketPool/IRocketStorage.sol';\nimport '../Interfaces/RocketPool/IMinipool.sol';\n\nimport '../Interfaces/IWETH.sol';\n\nimport './WETHVault.sol';\n\nimport './Utils/Constants.sol';\n\n/**\n * @title SuperNodeAccount\n * @author Theodore Clapp, Mike Leach\n * @dev Abstracts all created minipools under a single node\n */\ncontract SuperNodeAccount is UpgradeableBase {\n    event MinipoolCreated(address indexed minipoolAddress, address indexed operatorAddress);\n    event MinipoolStaked(address indexed minipoolAddress, address indexed operatorAddress);\n    event MinipoolDestroyed(address indexed minipoolAddress, address indexed operatorAddress);\n\n    // parameters\n    event MaxValidatorsChanged(uint256 indexed oldValue, uint256 indexed newValue);\n    event BondChanged(uint256 indexed oldValue, uint256 indexed newValue);\n    event MinimumNodeFeeChanged(uint256 indexed oldValue, uint256 indexed newValue);\n    event AllowSubNodeOperatorDelegateChangesChanged(bool indexed oldValue, bool indexed newValue);\n    event LockThresholdChanged(uint256 indexed oldLockThreshold, uint256 indexed newLockThreshold);\n    event AdminServerCheckChanged(bool indexed oldValue, bool indexed newValue);\n\n    // Mapping of minipool address to the amount of ETH locked\n    mapping(address => uint256) public lockedEth;\n\n    // Lock threshold amount in wei\n    uint256 public lockThreshold;\n\n    // Variables for admin server message checks (if enabled for minipool creation)\n    bool public adminServerCheck;\n    mapping(address => uint256) public nonces;\n    uint256 public nonce;\n\n    bool lazyInit;\n\n    // List of all minipools\n    address[] public minipools;\n\n    // FOR OFFCHAIN USE ONLY - DO NOT USE IN CONTRACTS\n    mapping(address => address[]) public __subNodeOperatorMinipools__;\n\n    struct Minipool {\n        address subNodeOperator;\n        uint256 ethTreasuryFee;\n        uint256 noFee;\n        uint256 index; // index in the minipool list\n    }\n\n    struct CreateMinipoolConfig {\n        bytes validatorPubkey;\n        bytes validatorSignature;\n        bytes32 depositDataRoot;\n        uint256 salt;\n        address expectedMinipoolAddress;\n        bytes sig;\n    }\n\n    // Mapping of address to minipool structs\n    mapping(address => Minipool) public minipoolData;\n\n    // admin settings\n    /// @notice Bond amount for newly created minipools\n    /// @dev ONLY use this for creating minipools. Do not use this for calculating rewards!\n    uint256 public bond;\n    uint256 public minimumNodeFee;\n    uint256 public maxValidators; // max number of validators each NO is allowed\n    bool public allowSubOpDelegateChanges;\n\n    /// @notice Modifier to ensure a function can only be called once for lazy initialization\n    modifier lazyInitializer() {\n        require(!lazyInit, 'already lazily initialized');\n        _;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Modifier to ensure a function can only be called by a sub-node operator of a specific minipool\n    modifier onlySubNodeOperator(address _minipool) {\n        require(minipoolData[_minipool].subNodeOperator == msg.sender, 'Can only be called by SubNodeOperator!');\n        _;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Modifier to ensure a function can only be called by a sub-node operator or admin of a specific minipool\n    modifier onlyAdminOrAllowedSNO(address _minipool) {\n        if (allowSubOpDelegateChanges) {\n            require(\n                _directory.hasRole(Constants.ADMIN_ROLE, msg.sender) ||\n                    minipoolData[_minipool].subNodeOperator == msg.sender,\n                'Can only be called by admin or sub node operator'\n            );\n        } else {\n            require(\n                _directory.hasRole(Constants.ADMIN_ROLE, msg.sender),\n                'Minipool delegate changes only allowed by admin'\n            );\n        }\n        _;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Modifier to ensure a function can only be called if the minipool has been configured\n    modifier onlyRecognizedMinipool(address _minipool) {\n        require(getIsMinipoolRecognized(_minipool), 'minipool not recognized');\n        _;\n    }\n\n    function getIsMinipoolRecognized(address minipool) public view returns (bool) {\n        return minipoolData[minipool].subNodeOperator != address(0);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Initializes the contract with the provided directory address.\n     * @dev This function should only be called once, during contract creation or proxy initialization.\n     * @param _directory The address of the directory contract.\n     */\n    function initialize(address _directory) public override initializer {\n        super.initialize(_directory);\n\n        adminServerCheck = true;\n        minimumNodeFee = 14e16;\n        bond = 8 ether;\n        maxValidators = 1;\n        allowSubOpDelegateChanges = false;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Performs lazy initialization of the contract.\n     */\n    function lazyInitialize() external lazyInitializer {\n        Directory directory = Directory(_directory);\n        IRocketNodeManager rnm = IRocketNodeManager(_directory.getRocketNodeManagerAddress());\n        rnm.registerNode('Australia/Brisbane');\n        address od = directory.getOperatorDistributorAddress();\n        IRocketStorage(directory.getRocketStorageAddress()).setWithdrawalAddress(address(this), od, true);\n        lockThreshold = IRocketDAOProtocolSettingsMinipool(getDirectory().getRocketDAOProtocolSettingsMinipool())\n            .getPreLaunchValue();\n        rnm.setSmoothingPoolRegistrationState(true);\n\n        lazyInit = true;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice This function is responsible for the creation and initialization of a minipool based on the validator's configuration.\n     *         It requires that the calling node operator is whitelisted and that the signature provided for the minipool creation is valid (if signature checks are enabled).\n     *         It also checks for sufficient liquidity (both RPL and ETH) before proceeding with the creation.\n     *         See the `CreateMinipoolConfig` struct for the parameters required for minipool creation.\n     * @dev The function involves multiple steps:\n     *      1. Validates that the transaction contains the exact amount of ETH specified in the `lockThreshold` (to prevent deposit contract front-running).\n     *      2. Checks if there is sufficient liquidity available for the required bond amount in both RPL and ETH.\n     *      3. Validates that the sender (sub-node operator) is whitelisted.\n     *      4. Ensures the signature provided has not been used before and marks it as used.\n     *      5. If admin server message checks are enabled, it verifies that the signature recovers to an address with the admin server role.\n     *      6. Ensures that the expected minipool address from the configuration has not been initialized before.\n     *      7. Locks the sent ETH, updates the total locked ETH count, and sets the timestamp when the lock started.\n     *      8. Adds the minipool to the tracking arrays and mappings.\n     *      9. Calls the `OperatorDistributor` to handle liquidity provisioning and event logging for the minipool creation.\n     *      10. Finally, it delegates the deposit to the `RocketNodeDeposit` contract with all the required parameters from the configuration.\n     */\n    function createMinipool(CreateMinipoolConfig calldata _config) public payable {\n        // this is the most common reason why minipools can't be created, so it should be checked first in this gas-sensitive function\n        require(hasSufficientLiquidity(bond), 'NodeAccount: protocol must have enough rpl and eth');\n        address subNodeOperator = msg.sender;\n        require(\n            Whitelist(_directory.getWhitelistAddress()).getIsAddressInWhitelist(subNodeOperator),\n            'sub node operator must be whitelisted'\n        );\n        require(\n            Whitelist(_directory.getWhitelistAddress()).getActiveValidatorCountForOperator(subNodeOperator) <\n                maxValidators,\n            'Sub node operator has created too many minipools already'\n        );\n        require(msg.value == lockThreshold, 'SuperNode: must set the message value to lockThreshold');\n        require(\n            IRocketMinipoolManager(_directory.getRocketMinipoolManagerAddress()).getMinipoolExists(\n                _config.expectedMinipoolAddress\n            ) == false,\n            'minipool already initialized'\n        );\n\n        uint256 salt = uint256(keccak256(abi.encodePacked(_config.salt, subNodeOperator)));\n        // verify admin server signature if required\n        if (adminServerCheck) {\n            address recoveredAddress = ECDSA.recover(\n                ECDSA.toEthSignedMessageHash(\n                    keccak256(\n                        abi.encodePacked(\n                            _config.expectedMinipoolAddress,\n                            salt,\n                            address(this),\n                            nonces[subNodeOperator]++,\n                            nonce,\n                            block.chainid\n                        )\n                    )\n                ),\n                _config.sig\n            );\n            require(\n                _directory.hasRole(Constants.ADMIN_SERVER_ROLE, recoveredAddress),\n                'bad signer role, params, or encoding'\n            );\n        }\n\n        // move the necessary ETH to this contract for use\n        OperatorDistributor od = OperatorDistributor(_directory.getOperatorDistributorAddress());\n        od.sendEthForMinipool();\n\n        lockedEth[_config.expectedMinipoolAddress] = msg.value;\n\n        minipools.push(_config.expectedMinipoolAddress);\n\n        WETHVault wethVault = WETHVault(getDirectory().getWETHVaultAddress());\n        minipoolData[_config.expectedMinipoolAddress] = Minipool(\n            subNodeOperator,\n            wethVault.treasuryFee(),\n            wethVault.nodeOperatorFee(),\n            minipools.length - 1\n        );\n\n        // register minipool with node operator\n        Whitelist(getDirectory().getWhitelistAddress()).registerNewValidator(subNodeOperator);\n\n        // stake additional RPL to cover the new minipool\n        od.rebalanceRplStake(getEthStaked() + bond);\n\n        // do the deposit!\n        IRocketNodeDeposit(_directory.getRocketNodeDepositAddress()).deposit{value: bond}(\n            bond,\n            minimumNodeFee,\n            _config.validatorPubkey,\n            _config.validatorSignature,\n            _config.depositDataRoot,\n            salt,\n            _config.expectedMinipoolAddress\n        );\n\n        __subNodeOperatorMinipools__[subNodeOperator].push(_config.expectedMinipoolAddress);\n\n        od.rebalanceWethVault();\n        od.rebalanceRplVault();\n\n        emit MinipoolCreated(_config.expectedMinipoolAddress, subNodeOperator);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Stops tracking a specified minipool by removing it from the active list.\n     * @dev Removes a minipool from the active tracking array and updates mappings to reflect this change.\n     *      This is used when a minipool is destroyed or decommissioned.\n     * @param minipool The address of the minipool to be removed from tracking.\n     */\n    function removeMinipool(address minipool) external onlyProtocol onlyRecognizedMinipool(address(minipool)) {\n        uint256 index = minipoolData[minipool].index;\n        // in case a minipool was dissolved or scrubbed, unlock the ETH and move it to the OD for later use\n        if (lockedEth[minipool] > 0) {\n            uint256 lockupBalance = lockedEth[minipool];\n            lockedEth[minipool] = 0;\n            (bool success, ) = getDirectory().getOperatorDistributorAddress().call{value: lockupBalance}('');\n            require(success, 'ETH transfer failed');\n        }\n        address operatorAddress = minipoolData[minipool].subNodeOperator;\n        uint256 lastIndex = minipools.length - 1;\n        address lastMinipool = minipools[lastIndex];\n        minipools[index] = lastMinipool;\n        minipoolData[lastMinipool].index = index;\n        minipools.pop();\n        delete minipoolData[minipool];\n\n        emit MinipoolDestroyed(minipool, operatorAddress);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Initiates the staking process for a specified minipool. Refunds the lock for the minipool when called.\n     * @dev Calls the `stake` method on the minipool contract with necessary parameters.\n     *      This function can only be called by the sub-node operator of the minipool and when the minipool is properly configured.\n     * @param _minipool The address of the minipool to initiate staking.\n     */\n    function stake(\n        bytes calldata _validatorSignature,\n        bytes32 _depositDataRoot,\n        address _minipool\n    ) external onlySubNodeOperator(_minipool) onlyRecognizedMinipool(_minipool) {\n        IMinipool minipool = IMinipool(_minipool);\n        minipool.stake(_validatorSignature, _depositDataRoot);\n\n        // Refund the locked ETH\n        if (lockedEth[_minipool] > 0) {\n            uint256 lockupBalance = lockedEth[_minipool];\n            lockedEth[_minipool] = 0;\n            (bool success, ) = msg.sender.call{value: lockupBalance}('');\n            require(success, 'ETH transfer failed');\n        }\n\n        emit MinipoolStaked(_minipool, msg.sender);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Closes a dissolved minipool and updates the tracking and financial records accordingly.\n     * @dev This is one of the two ways that minipools can be removed from the system (the other being exits or scrubs, which are handled\n     * by processMinipool). Calling this is necessary to ensure that the associated minipool records are updated and ETH is pulled back\n     * into the system.\n     * In future versions, it may be brought into minipool processing to automate the process, but there are a lot of base layer\n     * implications to consider before closing, and it would increase gas for the tick.\n     * @param minipoolAddress Address of the minipool to close.\n     */\n    function closeDissolvedMinipool(address minipoolAddress) external onlyRecognizedMinipool(minipoolAddress) {\n        IMinipool minipool = IMinipool(minipoolAddress);\n        Whitelist(getDirectory().getWhitelistAddress()).removeValidator(minipoolData[minipoolAddress].subNodeOperator);\n        this.removeMinipool(minipoolAddress);\n        minipool.close();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Allows admins to delegate an upgrade to the minipool's contract.\n     * @dev This function provides a mechanism for delegated upgrades of minipools, enhancing flexibility in maintenance and upgrades.\n     * @param _minipool Address of the minipool which is to be upgraded.\n     */\n    function minipoolDelegateUpgrade(\n        address _minipool\n    ) external onlyAdminOrAllowedSNO(_minipool) onlyRecognizedMinipool(_minipool) {\n        IMinipool minipool = IMinipool(_minipool);\n        minipool.delegateUpgrade();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Allows sub-node operators or admins to rollback a delegated upgrade.\n     * @dev Provides a rollback mechanism for previously delegated upgrades, ensuring that upgrades can be reversed if necessary.\n     * @param _minipool Address of the minipool whose upgrade is to be rolled back.\n     */\n    function minipoolDelegateRollback(\n        address _minipool\n    ) external onlyAdminOrAllowedSNO(_minipool) onlyRecognizedMinipool(_minipool) {\n        IMinipool minipool = IMinipool(_minipool);\n        minipool.delegateRollback();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Sets the delegation setting for a minipool to either use the latest delegate or not.\n     * @dev Allows node operators or admins to configure whether a minipool should follow the latest delegate or not.\n     * @param _setting Boolean indicating whether to use the latest delegate.\n     * @param _minipool Address of the minipool whose delegation setting is to be configured.\n     */\n    function setUseLatestMinipoolDelegate(\n        bool _setting,\n        address _minipool\n    ) external onlyAdminOrAllowedSNO(_minipool) onlyRecognizedMinipool(_minipool) {\n        IMinipool minipool = IMinipool(_minipool);\n        minipool.setUseLatestDelegate(_setting);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Enables or disables the protocol's participation in the Rocket Pool smoothing pool\n     * @dev Admin-only\n     */\n    function setSmoothingPoolParticipation(bool _useSmoothingPool) external onlyAdmin {\n        IRocketNodeManager(_directory.getRocketNodeManagerAddress()).setSmoothingPoolRegistrationState(\n            _useSmoothingPool\n        );\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Enables or disables the ability for sub node operators to change minipool delegates\n     * @dev Admin-only\n     */\n    function setAllowSubNodeOpDelegateChanges(bool newValue) external onlyAdmin {\n        require(\n            newValue != allowSubOpDelegateChanges,\n            'SuperNodeAccount: new allowSubOpDelegateChanges value must be different'\n        );\n        emit AllowSubNodeOperatorDelegateChangesChanged(allowSubOpDelegateChanges, newValue);\n        allowSubOpDelegateChanges = newValue;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Enables or disables the server-admin approved sigs for creating minipools\n     * @dev This function can only be called by an admin\n     */\n    function setAdminServerCheck(bool newValue) external onlyAdmin {\n        require(newValue != adminServerCheck, 'SuperNodeAccount: new adminServerCheck value must be different');\n        emit AdminServerCheckChanged(adminServerCheck, newValue);\n        adminServerCheck = newValue;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Sets a new lock threshold.\n     * @param _newLockThreshold The new lock threshold value in wei.\n     */\n    function setLockAmount(uint256 _newLockThreshold) external onlyShortTimelock {\n        require(_newLockThreshold != lockThreshold, 'SuperNodeAccount: new lock threshold value must be different');\n        emit LockThresholdChanged(lockThreshold, _newLockThreshold);\n        lockThreshold = _newLockThreshold;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @return uint256 The amount of ETH bonded with this node from WETHVault deposits.\n     */\n    function getEthStaked() public view returns (uint256) {\n        return IRocketNodeStaking(getDirectory().getRocketNodeStakingAddress()).getNodeETHProvided(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @return uint256 The amount of ETH matched with this node from the rETH deposit pool\n     */\n    function getEthMatched() public view returns (uint256) {\n        return IRocketNodeStaking(getDirectory().getRocketNodeStakingAddress()).getNodeETHMatched(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @return uint256 The amount of RPL staked on this node\n     */\n    function getRplStaked() public view returns (uint256) {\n        return IRocketNodeStaking(_directory.getRocketNodeStakingAddress()).getNodeRPLStake(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Checks if there is sufficient liquidity in the protocol to cover a specified bond amount.\n     * @dev This function helps ensure that there are enough resources (both RPL and ETH) available\n     * in the system to cover the bonds required for creating or operating a minipool.\n     * It is crucial for maintaining financial stability and operational continuity.\n     * @param _bond The bond amount in wei for which liquidity needs to be checked.\n     * @return bool Returns true if there is sufficient liquidity to cover the bond; false otherwise.\n     */\n    function hasSufficientLiquidity(uint256 _bond) public view returns (bool) {\n        // check ETH\n        address payable od = _directory.getOperatorDistributorAddress();\n        bool hasEnoughEth = od.balance >= _bond;\n        if(hasEnoughEth == false) return false;\n\n        // check RPL\n        IRocketNodeStaking rocketNodeStaking = IRocketNodeStaking(_directory.getRocketNodeStakingAddress());\n        uint256 rplStaking = rocketNodeStaking.getNodeRPLStake(address(this));\n        uint256 newEthBorrowed = IRocketDAOProtocolSettingsMinipool(_directory.getRocketDAOProtocolSettingsMinipool())\n            .getLaunchBalance() - _bond;\n        uint256 rplRequired = OperatorDistributor(od).calculateRplStakeShortfall(\n            rplStaking,\n            getEthMatched() + newEthBorrowed\n        );\n        return IERC20(_directory.getRPLAddress()).balanceOf(od) >= rplRequired;\n    }\n\n    // Must receive ETH from OD for staking during the createMinipool process (pre-staking minipools)\n    receive() external payable onlyProtocol {}\n\n    function getNumMinipools() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return minipools.length;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Sets the bond amount used for new minipools.\n     * @param _newBond The new bond amount in wei.\n     */\n    function setBond(uint256 _newBond) external onlyAdmin {\n        require(_newBond != bond, 'SuperNodeAccount: new bond value must be different');\n        emit BondChanged(bond, _newBond);\n        bond = _newBond;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Sets the minimum node fee used for new minipools.\n     * @param _newMinimumNodeFee The new minimum node fee.\n     */\n    function setMinimumNodeFee(uint256 _newMinimumNodeFee) external onlyAdmin {\n        require(_newMinimumNodeFee != minimumNodeFee, 'SuperNodeAccount: new minimumNodeFee value must be different');\n        emit MinimumNodeFeeChanged(minimumNodeFee, _newMinimumNodeFee);\n        minimumNodeFee = _newMinimumNodeFee;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Sets the maximum numbder of allowed validators for each operator.\n     * @param _maxValidators The maximum number of validators to be considered in the reward calculation.\n     * @dev This function can only be called by the protocol admin.\n     * Adjusting this parameter will change the reward distribution dynamics for validators.\n     */\n    function setMaxValidators(uint256 _maxValidators) public onlyAdmin {\n        require(_maxValidators != maxValidators, 'SuperNodeAccount: new maxValidators value must be different');\n        emit MaxValidatorsChanged(maxValidators, _maxValidators);\n        maxValidators = _maxValidators;\n    }\n\n    function invalidateAllOutstandingSigs() external onlyAdmin {\n        nonce++;\n    }\n\n    function invalidateSingleOustandingSig(address _nodeOperator) external onlyAdmin {\n        nonces[_nodeOperator]++;\n    }\n\n    // FOR OFFCHAIN USE ONLY - DO NOT USE IN CONTRACTS\n    /// @notice Get the complete minipool count for a sub-node operator, including removed minipools\n    /// @param _subNodeOperator The address of the sub-node operator\n    function getMinipoolCount(address _subNodeOperator) external view returns (uint256) {\n        return __subNodeOperatorMinipools__[_subNodeOperator].length;\n    }\n\n    // FOR OFFCHAIN USE ONLY - DO NOT USE IN CONTRACTS\n    /// @notice Get the complete minipool list for a sub-node operator,   return __subNodeOperatorMinipools__[_subNodeOperator];\n    }\n}\n"

);\n        require(_protocol.weth == address(0) && newProtocol.weth != address(0), Constants.INITIALIZATION_ERROR);\n        require(_treasury == address(0) && governance.treasury != address(0),         governance.timelockMed != address(0) &&\n                governance.timelockLong != address(0),\n            Constants.INITIALIZATION_ERROR\n        );\n        require(governance.admin != address(0), Constants.INITIALIZATION_ERROR);\n\n        // set up role admins\n        AccessControlUpgradeable.__AccessControl_init();\n        _setRoleAdmin(Constants.ADMIN_ROLE, _setRoleAdmin(Constants.TREASURER_ROLE, Constants.TREASURER_ROLE);\n        _setRoleAdmin(Constants.CORE_PROTOCOL_ROLE, Constants.TIMELOCK_LONG);\n        _setRoleAdmin(Constants.TIMELOCK_SHORT, Constants.TIMELOCK_SHORT);\n        _setRoleAdmin(Constants.TIMELOCK_MED, Constants.TIMELOCK_MED);\n        _setRoleAdmin(Constants.TIMELOCK_LONG, Constants.TIMELOCK_LONG);\n\n        // set role addresses\n        _grantRole(Constants.ADMIN_ROLE, governance.admin);\n        _grantRole(Constants.TREASURER_ROLE, governance.treasurer);\n        _grantRole(Constants.TIMELOCK_SHORT, governance.timelockShort);\n        _grantRole(Constants.TIMELOCK_MED, governance.timelockMed);\n        _grantRole(Constants.TIMELOCK_LONG, governance.timelockLong);\n        _grantRole(Constants.ADMIN_SERVER_ROLE, governance.adminServer);\n        _grantRole(Constants.ADMIN_ORACLE_ROLE, governance.adminOracle);\n\n        // Note that the protocol role should ONLY be operator rewards address)\n        // and it should also never be given to an EOA (e.g. don't give this to the ADMIN or TREASURER)\n        // This is the only list of contracts which should ever have this role:\n        _grantRole(Constants.CORE_PROTOCOL_ROLE, newProtocol.whitelist);\n        _grantRole(Constants.CORE_PROTOCOL_ROLE, newProtocol.wethVault);\n        _grantRole(Constants.CORE_PROTOCOL_ROLE, newProtocol.rplVault);\n        _grantRole(Constants.CORE_PROTOCOL_ROLE, newProtocol.operatorDistributor);\n        _grantRole(Constants.CORE_PROTOCOL_ROLE, newProtocol.merkleClaimStreamer);\n        _grantRole(Constants.CORE_PROTOCOL_ROLE, newProtocol.oracle);\n        _grantRole(Constants.CORE_PROTOCOL_ROLE, newProtocol.priceFetcher);\n        _grantRole(Constants.CORE_PROTOCOL_ROLE, newProtocol.superNode);\n\n        _treasury = governance.treasury;\n        _operatorReward = operatorReward;\n        _protocol = newProtocol;\n\n DO NOT CALL THIS TO UPGRADE THE IMPLEMENTATION CONTRACT!\n    /// @dev Will enforce that the new address implements the IConstellationOracle interface\n    /// Note that this does NOT upgrade the existing implementation contract.\n    /// Instead it changes the contract completely, including storage!\n    /// @param newOracle The new oracle contract address.\n    function setOracle(IConstellationOracle newOracle) public {\n        require(hasRole(Constants.TIMELOCK_LONG, msg.sender), Constants.TIMELOCK_LONG_ONLY_ERROR);\n        address newAddress = address(newOracle);\n        emit OracleAddressChanged(_protocol.oracle, newAddress);\n        _protocol.oracle = newAddress;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Updates all protocol contract proxy addresses in a single call. DO NOT CALL THIS TO UPGRADE THE IMPLEMENTATION CONTRACTS!\n    /// @param newProtocol A Protocol struct containing updated addresses of protocol contracts.\n    /// @dev This function allows an administrator to change all protocol contract proxy addresses simultaneously.\n    /// * Note that this function does NOT validate that the addresses provided differ from the current addresses at the contract completely, including storage!\n    function setAll(Protocol memory newProtocol) public {\n        require(hasRole(Constants.TIMELOCK_LONG, msg.sender), Constants.TIMELOCK_LONG_ONLY_ERROR);\n        emit AllContractAddressesChanged(_protocol, newProtocol);\n        _protocol = newProtocol;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Enables or disables sanctions /// @notice Checks if a single account is sanctioned.\n    /// @param _account The account to check.\n    /// @return bool indicating if the account is sanctioned.\n    function isSanctioned(address _account) public returns (bool) {\n        address[] memory accounts = new address[](1);\n        accounts[0] = _account;\n        return _checkSanctions(accounts);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Checks if two accounts are sanctioned.\n    /// @param _account1 The first account to check.\n    /// @param _account2 The second account to check.\n    /// @return bool indicating if the accounts are sanctioned.\n    function isSanctioned(address _account1, address _account2) public returns (bool) {\n        address[] memory accounts = new address[](2);\n        accounts[0] = _account1;\n        accounts[1] = _account2;\n        return _checkSanctions(accounts);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Checks if multiple accounts are sanctioned.\n    /// @param _accounts The array of accounts to check.\n    /// @return bool indicating if any of the accounts are sanctioned.\n    function isSanctioned(address[] memory _accounts) public returns (bool) {\n        return _checkSanctions(_accounts);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Internal function to check if any accounts are sanctioned.\n    /// @param _accounts The array of accounts to check.\n    /// @return bool indicating if any of the accounts are sanctioned.\n    function _checkSanctions(address[] memory _accounts) internal returns (bool) {\n        if (!_enabledSanctions) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        bool sanctioned = false;\n        for (uint i = 0; i < _accounts.length; i++) {\n            if (_accounts[i] != address(0) && ISanctions(_protocol.sanctions).isSanctioned(_accounts[i])) {\n                emit SanctionViolation(_accounts[i], tx.origin);\n                sanctioned = true;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n        if (sanctioned || ISanctions(_protocol.sanctions).isSanctioned(tx.origin)) {\n            emit SanctionViolationSingleOrigin(tx.origin);\n            return true;\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n}\n"

      "content": "// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL v3\n\n/**\n  *    /***        /***          /******                                  /**               /** /**             /**     /**\n  *   /**_/       |_  **        /**__  **                                | **              | **| **            | **    |__/\n  *  | **   /** /** | **       | **  \\__/  /******  /*******   /******* /******    /****** | **| **  /******  /******   /**  /******  /*******\n  *  /***  |__/|__/ | ***      | **       /**__  **| **__  ** /**_____/|_  **_/   /**__  **| **| ** |____  **|_  **_/  | ** /**__  **| **__  **\n  * |  **           | **       | **      | **  \\ **| **  \\ **|  ******   | **    | ********| **| **  /*******  | **    | **| **  \\ **| **  \\ **\n  *  \\ **   /** /** | **       | **    **| **  | **| **  | ** \\____  **  | ** /* | **_____/| **| ** /**__  **  | ** /* | **| **  | **| **  | **\n  *  |  ***|__/|__/***         |  ******||  ****** | **  | ** /*******   | ****  |  *******| **| **| ********  | ****  | **|  ****** | **  | **\n  *   \\___/       |___/         \\______/  \\______/ |__/  |__/|_______/    \\___/   \\_______/|__/|__/ \\_______/   \\___/  |__/ \\______/ |__/  |__/\n  *\n  *  A liquid staking protocol extending Rocket Pool.\n  *  Made w/ <3 by {::}\n  *\n  *  For more information, visit https://nodeset.io\n  *\n  *  @author Mike Leach (Wander), Nick Steinhilber (NickS), Theodore Clapp (mryamz), Joe Clapis (jcrtp), Huy Nguyen, Andy Rose (Barbalute)\n  *  @custom:security-info https://docs.nodeset.io/nodeset/security-notice\n  **/\n\npragma solidity 0.8.17;\n\nimport '@openzeppelin/contracts-upgradeable/token/ERC20/extensions/ERC4626Upgradeable.sol';\n\nimport './RPLVault.sol';\nimport './Utils/PriceFetcher.sol';\nimport './Utils/UpgradeableBase.sol';\nimport './MerkleClaimStreamer.sol';\nimport '../Interfaces/IConstellationOracle.sol';\nimport '../Interfaces/RocketPool/IRocketDepositPool.sol';\nimport '../Interfaces/IRateProvider.sol';\n\n/// @custom:security-contact info@nodeoperator.org\ncontract WETHVault is UpgradeableBase, ERC4626Upgradeable, IRateProvider {\n    using Math for uint256;\n\n    event MaxWethRplRatioChanged(uint256 indexed oldValue, uint256 indexed newValue);\n    event TreasuryFeeChanged(uint256 indexed oldValue, uint256 indexed newValue);\n    event NodeOperatorFeeChanged(uint256 indexed oldValue, uint256 indexed newValue);\n    event WETHLiquidityReservePercentChanged(uint256 indexed oldValue, uint256 indexed newValue);\n    event MintFeeChanged(uint256 indexed oldValue, uint256 indexed newValue);\n    event DepositsEnabledChanged(bool indexed oldValue, bool indexed newValue);\n    event DifferingSenderRecipientEnabledChanged(bool indexed oldValue, bool indexed newValue);\n\n    string constant NAME = 'Constellation ETH';\n    string constant SYMBOL = 'xrETH';\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Sets the liquidity reserve as a percentage of TVL. E.g. if set to 2% (0.02e18), then 2% of the\n     * ETH backing xrETH will be reserved for withdrawals. If the reserve is below maximum, it will be refilled before assets are\n     * put to work with the OperatorDistributor.\n     */\n    uint256 public liquidityReservePercent;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice the maximum percentage of ETH/RPL TVL allowed\n     * @dev this is a simple percentage, because the RPL TVL is calculated using its price in ETH\n     */\n    uint256 public maxWethRplRatio;\n\n    uint256 public treasuryFee; // Treasury fee in basis points\n    uint256 public nodeOperatorFee; // NO fee in basis points\n\n    // To prevent oracle sandwich attacks, there is a small fee charged on mint\n    // see the original issue for RP for more details: https://consensys.io/diligence/audits/2021/04/rocketpool/#rockettokenreth---sandwiching-opportunity-on-price-updates\n    uint256 public mintFee;\n\n    bool public depositsEnabled;\n\n    // can the recipient of a deposit be different than the caller? False by default for extra security\n    bool public differingSenderRecipientEnabled;\n\n    // prevent APR drag from large deposits that would only queue minipools\n    bool public queueableDepositsLimitEnabled;\n\n    uint256 public oracleUpdateThreshold;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Initializes the vault with necessary parameters and settings.\n     * @dev This function sets up the vault's token references, fee structures, and various configurations.\n     * It's intended to be called once after deployment.\n     * @param directoryAddress Address of the directory contract to reference other platform contracts.\n     * @param weth Address of the WETH token contract to be used in this vault.\n     */\n    function initializeVault(address directoryAddress, address weth) public virtual initializer {\n        super.initialize(directoryAddress);\n        ERC4626Upgradeable.__ERC4626_init(IERC20Upgradeable(weth));\n        ERC20Upgradeable.__ERC20_init(NAME, SYMBOL);\n\n        liquidityReservePercent = 0.1e18; // 10% of TVL\n        maxWethRplRatio = 4e18; // 400% at start (4 ETH of xrETH for 1 ETH of xRPL)\n\n        // default fees with 14% rETH commission mean WETHVault share returns are equal to base ETH staking rewards\n        treasuryFee = 0.14788e18;\n        nodeOperatorFee = 0.14788e18;\n        mintFee = 0.0003e18; // .03% by default\n        depositsEnabled = true;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Reinitializer function to allow updates on contract upgrades specifically related to oracle update threshold\n     */\n    function reinitialize101() public reinitializer(2) {\n        // This can be called on upgrade to set new values\n        oracleUpdateThreshold = 88200; // 24.5 hrs in seconds\n        queueableDepositsLimitEnabled = true;\n    }\n\n\n    function calculateQueueableDepositLimit() public view returns (uint256) {\n        uint snaBond = SuperNodeAccount(_directory.getSuperNodeAddress()).bond();\n        \n        uint availableREth = IRocketDepositPool(_directory.getRocketDepositPoolAddress()).getExcessBalance();\n        // if there's no rETH available, queuable deposit limit is 0\n        if(availableREth == 0) return 0;\n\n        // this is the amount of ETH from constellation that can be paired with the available rETH\n        uint256 pairableEth = availableREth / ((32 ether - snaBond) / snaBond);\n        uint odBalance = address(_directory.getOperatorDistributorAddress()).balance;\n\n        // if there's already more than enough ETH in the OD to match, queuable deposit limit is 0\n        if(pairableEth <= odBalance) return 0;\n        return pairableEth - odBalance;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Handles deposits into the vault, ensuring compliance with RPL coverage ratio and distribution of fees.\n     * @dev This function first checks if the RPL coverage ratio is below the maximum threshold, and then continues with the deposit process.\n     * It updates the depositor's position, and distributes the assets to the OperatorDistributor for utilization. Also processes a minipool\n     * and rebalances ETH & WETH liquidity.\n     * @param caller The address initiating the deposit.\n     * @param receiver The address designated to receive the issued shares for the deposit.\n     * @param assets The amount of assets being deposited.\n     * @param shares The number of shares to be exchanged for the deposit.\n     */\n    function _deposit(\n        address caller,\n        address receiver,\n        uint256 assets,\n        uint256 shares\n    ) internal virtual override nonReentrant {\n        require(depositsEnabled, \"deposits are disabled\"); // emergency switch for deposits\n        require(differingSenderRecipientEnabled || caller == receiver, 'caller must be receiver');\n        require(!_directory.isSanctioned(caller, receiver), \"WETHVault: cannot deposit from or to a sanctioned address\");\n\n        uint256 lastOracleUpdate = IConstellationOracle(_directory.getOracleAddress()).getLastUpdatedTotalYieldAccrued();\n        require(block.timestamp <= lastOracleUpdate + oracleUpdateThreshold, \"WETHVault: Oracle is out of date.\");\n        if(queueableDepositsLimitEnabled) {\n            require(assets <= calculateQueueableDepositLimit(), \"WETHVault: Deposit exceeds the TVL queueable limit.\");\n        }\n\n        OperatorDistributor od = OperatorDistributor(_directory.getOperatorDistributorAddress());\n\n        require(tvlRatioEthRpl(assets, true) <= maxWethRplRatio, 'insufficient RPL coverage');\n\n        uint256 mintFeePortion = getMintFeePortion(assets);\n\n        super._deposit(caller, receiver, assets, shares);\n\n        address treasuryAddress = getDirectory().getTreasuryAddress();\n        if(mintFeePortion > 0 && treasuryAddress != address(this))\n            SafeERC20.safeTransfer(IERC20(asset()), treasuryAddress, mintFeePortion); // transfer the mint fee to the treasury\n\n        // move everything to operator distributor in anticipation of rebalancing everything\n        SafeERC20.safeTransfer(IERC20(asset()), address(od), IERC20(asset()).balanceOf(address(this)));\n        od.processNextMinipool();\n        od.rebalanceWethVault(); // just in case there is no minipool balances to process, rebalance anyway\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Handles withdrawals from the vault.\n     * @dev This function distributes the assets to the receiver and also transfers the assets from the OperatorDistributor as necessary.\n     * May revert if the liquidity reserves are too low. Processes a minipool before withdrawing, then rebalances the protocol's ETH & WETH liquidity.\n     * @param caller The address initiating the withdrawal.\n     * @param receiver The address designated to receive the withdrawn assets.\n     * @param owner The address that owns the shares being redeemed.\n     * @param assets The amount of assets being withdrawn.\n     * @param shares The number of shares to be burned in exchange for the withdrawal.\n     */\n    function _withdraw(\n        address caller,\n        address receiver,\n        address owner,\n        uint256 assets,\n        uint256 shares\n    ) internal virtual override nonReentrant {\n        require(caller == receiver, 'caller must be receiver');\n\n        OperatorDistributor od = OperatorDistributor(_directory.getOperatorDistributorAddress());\n        // first process a minipool to give the best chance at actually withdrawing\n        od.processNextMinipool();\n        require(IERC20(asset()).balanceOf(address(this)) >= assets, 'Not enough liquidity to withdraw');\n\n        // required violation of CHECKS/EFFECTS/INTERACTIONS: need to change WETH balance here before rebalancing the rest of the protocol\n        super._withdraw(caller, receiver, owner, assets, shares);\n\n        // rebalance again just in case there are no minipools to process, which skips the rebalance call in that function\n        od.rebalanceWethVault();\n    }\n\n    function _transfer(\n        address sender,\n        address recipient,\n        uint256 shares\n    ) internal virtual override {\n        require(!_directory.isSanctioned(sender, recipient), \"WETHVault: transfer not allowed from or to sanctioned address\");\n        super._transfer(sender, recipient, shares);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Preview taking an entry fee on deposit. See {IERC4626-previewDeposit}.\n    /// @return The amount of shares that will be received with the deposit amount specified.\n    function previewDeposit(uint256 assets) public view virtual override returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 fee = getMintFeePortion(assets);\n        return super.previewDeposit(assets - fee);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Preview adding an entry fee on mint. See {IERC4626-previewMint}.\n    /// @return The amount of assets that must be deposited to receive the shares specified.\n    function previewMint(uint256 shares) public view virtual override returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 assets = super.previewMint(shares);\n        return assets + getAdditionalMintFeeToReceive(assets);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Retrieves the total yield available for distribution.\n     * @dev This function calculates the yield that can be distributed by subtracting the total yield already distributed\n     * from the total yield accrued as reported by the Oracle.\n     * @return distributableYield The total yield available for distribution.\n     */\n    function getDistributableYield() public view returns (uint256 distributableYield, bool signed) {\n        int256 oracleError = int256(OperatorDistributor(_directory.getOperatorDistributorAddress()).oracleError());\n        int256 outstandingYield = (IConstellationOracle(getDirectory().getOracleAddress())).getTotalYieldAccrued();\n        if(outstandingYield == 0)\n            return (0, false);\n        // if the most recent reported yield is less than the oracleError, there's no unrealized yield remaining\n        int256 totalUnrealizedAccrual = outstandingYield >= 0 ? outstandingYield - oracleError : outstandingYield + oracleError;\n\n        int256 diff = totalUnrealizedAccrual;\n        if (diff >= 0) {\n            signed = false;\n            distributableYield = uint256(diff);\n        } else {\n            signed = true;\n            distributableYield = uint256(-diff);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Returns the total assets managed by this vault. That is, all the ETH backing xrETH.\n     * @dev This function calculates the total assets by summing the vault's own assets, the distributable yield,\n     * and the assets held in OperatorDistributor.\n     * @return The aggregated total assets managed by this vault.\n     */\n    function totalAssets() public view override returns (uint256) {\n        OperatorDistributor od = OperatorDistributor(getDirectory().getOperatorDistributorAddress());\n        (uint256 distributableYield, bool signed) = getDistributableYield();\n        uint256 merkleRewards = MerkleClaimStreamer(getDirectory().getMerkleClaimStreamerAddress()).getStreamedTvlEth();\n        return (\n            uint256(\n                int(IERC20(asset()).balanceOf(address(this)) + od.getTvlEth() +  merkleRewards)  +\n                    (signed ? -int(distributableYield) : int(distributableYield))\n            )\n        );\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculates the Total Value Locked (TVL) ratio between ETH and RPL within Constellation.\n     * @dev This function computes the ratio of the total value locked in ETH to the total value locked in RPL.\n     * It first retrieves the TVLs in ETH and RPL, then calculates the price of ETH in RPL units using a PriceFetcher.\n     * The ratio is given by (TVL in ETH * ETH price in RPL) / TVL in RPL. If TVL in RPL is 0, it returns a predefined\n     * ratio of 1e18 to handle division by zero errors.\n     * @return uint256 The ratio of TVL in ETH to TVL in RPL, scaled by 1e18.\n     */\n    function tvlRatioEthRpl(uint256 newDeposit, bool isWeth) public view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 tvlEth = totalAssets();\n        uint256 tvlRpl = RPLVault(getDirectory().getRPLVaultAddress()).totalAssets();\n\n        if (tvlRpl == 0) return 1e18;\n\n        uint256 rplPerEth = PriceFetcher(getDirectory().getPriceFetcherAddress()).getPrice();\n\n        return isWeth ? ((tvlEth + newDeposit) * rplPerEth) / tvlRpl : (tvlEth * rplPerEth) / (tvlRpl + newDeposit);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Convenience function for determining if a particular deposit amount will be allowed or rejected\n     */\n    function getIsDepositAllowed(uint256 amount) public view returns (bool) {\n        return tvlRatioEthRpl(amount, true) <= maxWethRplRatio;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Convenience function for determining if a particular withdraw amount will be allowed or rejected\n     */\n    function getIsWithdrawAllowed(uint256 amount) public view returns (bool) {\n        return IERC20(asset()).balanceOf(address(this)) >= amount;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Convenience function for viewing the maximum withdrawal allowed\n     */\n    function getMaximumWithdrawAmount() public view returns (uint256) {\n        return IERC20(asset()).balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Convenience function for viewing the maximum deposit allowed\n     */\n    function getMaximumDeposit() public view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 lastOracleUpdate = IConstellationOracle(_directory.getOracleAddress()).getLastUpdatedTotalYieldAccrued();\n        if(block.timestamp > lastOracleUpdate + oracleUpdateThreshold) return 0;\n        \n        uint queuableMax = type(uint256).max;\n        if(queueableDepositsLimitEnabled)\n            queuableMax = calculateQueueableDepositLimit();\n\n        RPLVault rplVault = RPLVault(getDirectory().getRPLVaultAddress());\n        if(tvlRatioEthRpl(0, false) < rplVault.minWethRplRatio()) return 0;\n\n        uint256 tvlRpl = rplVault.totalAssets();\n        uint256 tvlEth = totalAssets();\n        uint256 rplPerEth = PriceFetcher(getDirectory().getPriceFetcherAddress()).getPrice();\n\n        uint ratioMax = 0;\n        if(rplPerEth == 0)\n            ratioMax = type(uint256).max;\n        else\n            ratioMax = ((maxWethRplRatio * tvlRpl) / rplPerEth) - tvlEth;\n            \n        return queuableMax > ratioMax ? ratioMax : queuableMax;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculates the missing liquidity needed to meet the liquidity reserve after a specified deposit.\n     * @dev Compares the current balance with the required liquidity based on the total assets including the deposit and mint fee.\n     * @param deposit The amount of the new deposit to consider in the liquidity calculation.\n     * @return The amount of liquidity required after the specified deposit.\n     */\n    function getMissingLiquidityAfterDeposit(uint256 deposit) public view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 fullBalance = totalAssets() + deposit - getMintFeePortion(deposit);\n        uint256 currentBalance = IERC20(asset()).balanceOf(address(this));\n        uint256 requiredBalance = liquidityReservePercent.mulDiv(fullBalance, 1e18, Math.Rounding.Up);\n        return requiredBalance > currentBalance ? requiredBalance - currentBalance: 0;\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Calculates the missing liquidity needed to meet the liquidity reserve after a specified deposit without considering the mint fee.\n    function getMissingLiquidityAfterDepositNoFee(uint256 deposit) public view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 fullBalance = totalAssets() + deposit;\n        uint256 currentBalance = IERC20(asset()).balanceOf(address(this));\n        uint256 requiredBalance = liquidityReservePercent.mulDiv(fullBalance, 1e18, Math.Rounding.Up);\n        return requiredBalance > currentBalance ? requiredBalance - currentBalance: 0;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculates the missing liquidity needed to meet the liquidity reserve.\n     * @dev This function calculates the current assets needed to hit the liquidity reserve\n     * based the current total assets of the vault.\n     * @return The amount of liquidity required.\n     */\n    function getMissingLiquidity() public view returns (uint256) {\n        return getMissingLiquidityAfterDepositNoFee(0);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculates the xrETH portion of rewards for a specified amount of income\n     */\n    function getIncomeAfterFees(uint256 income) public view returns (uint256){\n        return income - income.mulDiv((treasuryFee + nodeOperatorFee), 1e18);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Calculates the fee assets that must be added to make a deposit of `_amount` size.\n    /// @notice Calculates the mint fee portion of a specific deposit amount.\n    /// @param _amount The deposit expected\n    function getAdditionalMintFeeToReceive(uint256 _amount) public view returns (uint256) {\n        return _amount.mulDiv(mintFee, 1e18, Math.Rounding.Up);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Calculates the mint fee portion of a specific deposit amount.\n    /// @param _amount The desired deposit amount\n    function getMintFeePortion(uint256 _amount) public view returns (uint256) {\n        return _amount.mulDiv(mintFee, mintFee + 1e18, Math.Rounding.Up);\n    }\n\n    /// Calculates the treasury portion of a specific RPL reward amount.\n    /// @param _amount The RPL reward expected\n    function getTreasuryPortion(uint256 _amount) external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _amount.mulDiv(treasuryFee, 1e18);\n    }\n\n    /// Calculates the operator portion of a specific RPL reward amount.\n    /// @param _amount The RPL reward expected\n    function getOperatorPortion(uint256 _amount) external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _amount.mulDiv(nodeOperatorFee, 1e18);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Shortcut for easier defi integration (e.g. Balancer)\n    /// @return The value of 1 xrETH in terms of WETH\n    function getRate() public view returns (uint256) {\n        return convertToAssets(1 ether);\n    }\n\n    /**ADMIN FUNCTIONS */\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Sets whether the recipient of a deposit is allowed to be different than the caller of the deposit function.\n     * @param _newValue The new value for the differingSenderRecipientEnabled variable\n     */\n    function setDifferingSenderRecipientEnabled(bool _newValue) external onlyAdmin {\n        require(_newValue != differingSenderRecipientEnabled, 'WETHVault: new differingSenderRecipientEnabled value must be different than existing value');\n        emit DifferingSenderRecipientEnabledChanged(differingSenderRecipientEnabled, _newValue);\n        differingSenderRecipientEnabled = _newValue;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Sets the minimum ETH/RPL coverage ratio for the vault.\n     * @dev This function allows the admin to update the RPL coverage ratio, which determines the\n     * minimum ETH/RPL coverage required for the vault's health.\n     * @param _maxWethRplRatio The new ETH/RPL coverage ratio to be set.\n     */\n    function setMaxWethRplRatio(uint256 _maxWethRplRatio) external onlyShortTimelock {\n        require(_maxWethRplRatio != maxWethRplRatio, 'WETHVault: new maxWethRplRatio value must be different than existing value');\n        emit MaxWethRplRatioChanged(maxWethRplRatio, _maxWethRplRatio);\n        maxWethRplRatio = _maxWethRplRatio;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Sets the treasury fee.\n     * @dev This function allows the admin to update the treasury fee.\n     * This fee and the total fee must not exceed 100%.\n     * @param _treasuryFee The new treasury fee (1e18 = 100%).\n     */\n    function setTreasuryFee(uint256 _treasuryFee) external onlyMediumTimelock {\n        require(_treasuryFee + nodeOperatorFee <= 1e18, 'Total fees cannot exceed 100%');\n        require(_treasuryFee != treasuryFee, 'WETHVault: new treasury fee value must be different than existing value');\n        emit TreasuryFeeChanged(treasuryFee, _treasuryFee);\n        treasuryFee = _treasuryFee;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Sets the node operator fee.\n     * @dev This function allows the admin to update the node operator fee.\n     * This fee and the total fee must not exceed 100%.\n     * @param _nodeOperatorFee The new node operator fee (1e18 = 100%).\n     */\n    function setNodeOperatorFee(uint256 _nodeOperatorFee) external onlyMediumTimelock {\n        require(treasuryFee + _nodeOperatorFee <= 1e18, 'Total fees cannot exceed 100%');\n        require(_nodeOperatorFee != nodeOperatorFee, 'WETHVault: new operator fee must be different than existing value');\n        emit NodeOperatorFeeChanged(nodeOperatorFee, _nodeOperatorFee);\n        nodeOperatorFee = _nodeOperatorFee;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Sets protocol fees.\n     * @dev This function allows the admin to update the node operator fee.\n     * The total fee must not exceed 100%. (1e18 = 100%)\n     * @param _nodeOperatorFee The new node operator fee.\n     * @param _treasuryFee The new treasury fee.\n     */\n    function setProtocolFees(uint256 _nodeOperatorFee, uint256 _treasuryFee) external onlyMediumTimelock {\n        require(_treasuryFee + _nodeOperatorFee <= 1e18, 'Total fees cannot exceed 100%');\n\n        require(_nodeOperatorFee != nodeOperatorFee, 'WETHVault: new operator fee must be different than existing value');\n        emit NodeOperatorFeeChanged(nodeOperatorFee, _nodeOperatorFee);\n\n        require(_treasuryFee != treasuryFee, 'WETHVault: new treasury fee value must be different than existing value');\n        emit TreasuryFeeChanged(treasuryFee, _treasuryFee);\n\n        nodeOperatorFee = _nodeOperatorFee;\n        treasuryFee = _treasuryFee;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Sets the liquidity reserve as a percentage of TVL. E.g. if set to 2% (0.02e18), then 2% of the\n     * ETH backing xrETH will be reserved for withdrawals. If the reserve is below maximum, it will be refilled before assets are\n     * put to work with the OperatorDistributor.\n     * @dev This function allows the admin to update the liquidity reserve which determines the amount available for withdrawals.\n     * The liquidity reserve must be a reasonable percentage between 0 and 100%. 1e18 = 100%\n     * @param _liquidityReservePercent The new liquidity reserve percentage.\n     * @custom:requires This function can only be called by an address with the Medium Timelock role.\n     */\n    function setLiquidityReservePercent(uint256 _liquidityReservePercent) external onlyShortTimelock {\n        require(_liquidityReservePercent <= 1e18, 'WETHVault: liquidity reserve percentage must be between 0% and 100% (1e18)');\n        require(_liquidityReservePercent != liquidityReservePercent, 'WETHVault: new liquidity reserve percentage value must be different than existing value');\n        emit WETHLiquidityReservePercentChanged(liquidityReservePercent, _liquidityReservePercent);\n        liquidityReservePercent = _liquidityReservePercent;\n\n        // rebalance entire balance of the contract to ensure the new liquidity reserve is respected\n        OperatorDistributor od = OperatorDistributor(getDirectory().getOperatorDistributorAddress());\n        SafeERC20.safeTransfer(IERC20(asset()), address(od), IERC20(asset()).balanceOf(address(this)));\n        od.rebalanceWethVault();\n    }\n\n    function setMintFee(uint256 newMintFee) external onlyMediumTimelock() {\n        require(newMintFee <= 1e18, \"WETHVault: new mint fee must be between 0% and 100% (1e18)\");\n        require(newMintFee != mintFee, 'WETHVault: new mint fee value must be different than existing value');\n        emit MintFeeChanged(mintFee, newMintFee);\n        mintFee = newMintFee;\n    }\n\n    function setDepositsEnabled(bool _newValue) external onlyAdmin {\n        require(_newValue != depositsEnabled, 'WETHVault: new depositsEnabled value must be different than existing value');\n        emit DepositsEnabledChanged(depositsEnabled, _newValue);\n        depositsEnabled = _newValue;\n    }\n\n    function setQueueableDepositsLimitEnabled(bool _newValue) external onlyAdmin {\n        require(_newValue != queueableDepositsLimitEnabled, 'WETHVault: new queueableDepositsLimitEnabled value must be different than existing value');\n        queueableDepositsLimitEnabled = _newValue;\n    }\n\n    function setOracleUpdateThreshold(uint256 _newValue) external onlyAdmin {\n        require(_newValue != oracleUpdateThreshold, 'WETHVault: new oracleUpdateThreshold value must be different than existing value');\n        oracleUpdateThreshold = _newValue;\n    }\n}"

 \\ **   /** /** | **       | **    **| **  | **| **  | ** \\____  **  | ** /* | **_____/| **| ** /**__  **  | ** /* | **| **  | **| **  | **\n  *  |  ***|__/|__/***         |  ******||  ****** | **  | ** /*******   | ****  |  *******| **| **| ********  | ****  | **|  ****** | **  | **\n  *   \\___/       |___/         \\______/  \\______/ |__/  |__/|_______/    \\___/   \\_______/|__/|__/ \\_______/   \\___/  |__/ \\______/ |__/  |__/\n  *\n  *  A liquid staking protocol extending Rocket Pool.\n  *  Made w/ <3 by {::}\n  *\n  *  For more information, visit https://nodeset.io\n  *\n  *  @author Mike Leach (Wander), Nick Steinhilber (NickS), Theodore Clapp (mryamz), Joe Clapis (jcrtp), Huy Nguyen, Andy Rose (Barbalute)\n  *  @custom:security-info https://docs.nodeset.io/nodeset/security-notice\n  **/\n\npragma solidity 0.8.17;\n\nimport '@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/cryptography/ECDSA.sol';\n\nimport './Utils/UpgradeableBase.sol';\nimport './SuperNodeAccount.sol';\nimport './Utils/Constants.sol';\n\n/// @notice An operator which provides services to the network.\nstruct Operator {\n    uint256 activeValidatorCount;\n    bool allowed;\n    uint256 nonce;\n}\n\n/**\n * @title Whitelist\n * @author Mike Leach, Theodore Clapp\n * @notice Controls operator access to the protocol. Only modifiable by permission of the admin.\n */ \ncontract Whitelist is UpgradeableBase {\n    event OperatorAdded(Operator);\n    event OperatorsAdded(address[] operators);\n\n    event OperatorRemoved(address);\n    event OperatorsRemoved(address[] operators);\n\n    mapping(address => Operator) public operators;\n    uint256 public nonce;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Initializes the Whitelist contract with a directory address.\n     * @param directoryAddress The address of the directory contract.\n     */\n    function initializeWhitelist(address directoryAddress) public initializer {\n        super.initialize(directoryAddress);\n    }\n\n    //----\n    // GETTERS\n    //----\n\n    /// @notice Checks if an address is in the whitelist.\n    /// @dev This function allows users to query whether a specific address is in the whitelist.\n    /// @param a The address to check.\n    /// @return True if the address is in the whitelist, otherwise false.\n    function getIsAddressInWhitelist(address a) public view returns (bool) {\n        return operators[a].allowed;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Retrieves the Operator struct associated with a specific address.\n    /// @dev This function allows users to retrieve detailed information about an operator\n    ///      based on their address.\n    /// @param a The address of the operator to retrieve information for.\n    /// @return An Operator struct containing details about the operator.\n    /// @dev Throws an error if the specified address is not found in the whitelist.\n    function getOperatorAtAddress(address a) public view returns (Operator memory) {\n        return operators[a];\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Retrieves the number of validators associated with a specific operator address.\n    /// @dev This function allows users to query the number of validators managed by an operator\n    ///      based on their address.\n    /// @param a The address of the operator to retrieve the number of validators for.\n    /// @return The number of validators associated with the specified operator.\n    /// @dev Throws an error if the specified address is not found in the whitelist.\n    function getActiveValidatorCountForOperator(address a) public view returns (uint) {\n        return operators[a].activeValidatorCount;\n    }\n\n    function getNonceForOperator(address a) public view returns (uint) {\n        return operators[a].nonce;\n    }\n\n    //----\n    // INTERNAL\n    //----\n\n    /// @notice Registers a new validator under a specific node operator.\n    /// @dev This function allows the protocol to register a new validator under a node operator's address.\n    /// @param operatorAddress The address of the node operator to register the new validator under.\n    /// @dev Increases the validator count for the specified node operator.\n    function registerNewValidator(address operatorAddress) public onlyProtocol {\n        operators[operatorAddress].activeValidatorCount++;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Registers a new validator under a specific node operator.\n    /// @dev This function allows the protocol to register a new validator under a node operator's address.\n    /// @param operatorAddress The address of the node operator the validator is being removed from.\n    /// @dev Decreases the validator count for the specified node operator.\n    function removeValidator(address operatorAddress) public onlyProtocol {\n        // ensure this validator is associated with an operator\n        // if it's not, then the operator may have been removed, so just do nothing\n        if(operators[operatorAddress].activeValidatorCount == 0) {\n            return;\n        }\n        operators[operatorAddress].activeValidatorCount--;\n    }\n\n    //----\n    // PUBLIC\n    //----\n\n    /// @notice Adds a new operator to the whitelist.\n    /// @dev Ensures that the operator being added is not a duplicate.\n    ///      It emits the 'OperatorAdded' event to notify when an operator has been successfully added.\n    /// @param operatorAddress The address of the operator to be added.\n    /// @dev Throws an error if the operator being added already exists in the whitelist.\n    function addOperator(address operatorAddress, bytes calldata sig) public {\n        emit OperatorAdded(_addOperator(operatorAddress, sig));\n    }\n\n    //----\n    // ADMIN\n    //----\n\n    /// @notice Internal function to remove an operator from the whitelist.\n    /// @dev This function is used internally to remove an operator from the whitelist, including updating permissions, clearing operator data,\n    ///      and notifying the OperatorDistributor and NodeSetOperatorRewardDistributor contracts.\n    /// @param operatorAddress The address of the operator to be removed.\n    function _removeOperator(address operatorAddress) internal {\n        operators[operatorAddress].allowed = false;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Removes an operator from the whitelist.\n    /// @dev This function can only be called by a 24-hour timelock and is used to remove an operator from the whitelist.\n    ///      It emits the 'OperatorRemoved' event to notify when an operator has been successfully removed.\n    /// @param nodeOperator The address of the operator to be removed.\n    function removeOperator(address nodeOperator) public onlyShortTimelock {\n        _removeOperator(nodeOperator);\n        emit OperatorRemoved(nodeOperator);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Batch addition of operators to the whitelist.\n    /// @dev This function can only be called by a 24-hour timelock and allows multiple operators to be added to the whitelist simultaneously.\n    ///      It checks for duplicates among the provided addresses, adds valid operators, and emits the 'OperatorsAdded' event.\n    /// @param operatorAddresses An array of addresses representing the operators to be added.\n    /// @dev Throws an error if any of the operators being added already exist in the whitelist.\n    function addOperators(address[] memory operatorAddresses, bytes[] memory _sig) public {\n        for (uint i = 0; i < operatorAddresses.length; i++) {\n            _addOperator(operatorAddresses[i], _sig[i]);\n        }\n        emit OperatorsAdded(operatorAddresses);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Batch removal of operators from the whitelist.\n    /// @dev This function can only be called by a 24-hour timelock and allows multiple operators to be removed from the whitelist simultaneously.\n    ///      It removes valid operators and emits the 'OperatorsRemoved' event.\n    /// @param operatorAddresses An array of addresses representing the operators to be removed.\n    /// @dev Throws no errors during execution.\n    function removeOperators(address[] memory operatorAddresses) public onlyShortTimelock {\n        for (uint i = 0; i < operatorAddresses.length; i++) {\n            _removeOperator(operatorAddresses[i]);\n        }\n        emit OperatorsRemoved(operatorAddresses);\n    }\n\n    //----\n    // INTERNAL\n    //----\n\n    /// @notice Internal function to add a new operator to the whitelist. Signs an address; optimized for server-side signatures with isolated scope on Ethereum.\n    /// @dev This function is used internally to add a new operator to the whitelist, including updating permissions, initializing operator data,\n    /// and emitting the 'OperatorAdded' event. This method signs the address directly, which is typically discouraged due to potential risks\n    /// of address reuse across different protocols or chains. However, for this specific application where\n    /// the signer is only the server and its operational scope is limited to this Ethereum deployment,\n    /// it is considered safe and more optimal. Signatures are completely isolated to this scope.\n    /// @param _operator The address of the operator to be added.\n    /// @return An Operator struct containing details about the newly added operator.\n    function _addOperator(\n        address _operator,\n        bytes memory _sig\n    ) internal returns (Operator memory) {\n        require(!this.getIsAddressInWhitelist(_operator), Constants.OPERATOR_DUPLICATE_ERROR);\n        \n        bytes32 messageHash = keccak256(abi.encodePacked(_operator, address(this), operators[_operator].nonce++, nonce, block.chainid));\n        bytes32 ethSignedMessageHash = ECDSA.toEthSignedMessageHash(messageHash);\n        address recoveredAddress = ECDSA.recover(ethSignedMessageHash, _sig);\n        require(_directory.hasRole(Constants.ADMIN_SERVER_ROLE, recoveredAddress), 'bad signature');\n\n        operators[_operator].allowed = true;\n\n        return operators[_operator];\n    }\n\n    function invalidateAllOutstandingSigs() external onlyAdmin {\n        nonce++;\n    }\n\n    function invalidateSingleOutstandingSig(address _nodeOperator) external onlyAdmin {\n        operators[_nodeOperator].nonce++;\n    }\n}\n"
